		private void txtMarkerLabel_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(txtMarkerLabel.Text))
			{
				btnOK.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				btnOK.IsEnabled = true;
			}
		}

		{
			base.DialogResult = true;
			Close();
		}

		private void btnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DialogResult = false;
			Close();
		}

		public AzeoAnalysisModule(IUnityContainer container, IScreenFactoryCatalog screenFactoryCatalog)
		{
			if (container == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("container");
			}
			if (screenFactoryCatalog == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("screenFactoryCatalog");
			}
			Container = container;
			ScreenFactoryCatalog = screenFactoryCatalog;
		}

		public void Initialize()
		{
			ScreenFactoryCatalog.RegisterScreenFactory("SplitAzeoAnalysisKey", typeof(AzeotropeAnalysisScreen));
			ScreenFactoryCatalog.RegisterScreenFactory("SplitTernaryMapKey", typeof(TernaryMapScreen));
		}
	}

	public class AzeotropeAnalysisScreen : ApwnDocumentControl, INotifyPropertyChanged, IComponentConnector
	{
		private bool _hasLoaded;

		private string _strHelpFile = "streamana.chm";

		private string _strHelpID = "1";

		public CAzeotropeSearch m_objAzeotropeSearch = new CAzeotropeSearch();

		private ObservableCollection<SplitTreeNode> _treeNodeList = new ObservableCollection<SplitTreeNode>();

		internal TreeView explorerTree;

		internal UserControl rightViewPanel;

		internal Grid theContainerGrid;

		internal UsrAzeoInput pageAzeoInput;

		internal UserCompositionGrid pagePureComponent;

		internal UserCompositionGrid pageAzeoComposition;

		internal UserCompositionGrid pageSingular;

		internal UsrAzeoReport pageAzeoReport;

		internal MMInContextGuidanceButton buttonInContextGuidance;

		internal TextBlock txtStatus;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public IUnityContainer Container { get; private set; }

		public string HelpFile
		{
			get
			{
				return _strHelpFile;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_strHelpFile != value)
				{
					_strHelpFile = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("HelpFile");
				}
			}
		}

		public string HelpID
		{
			get
			{
				return _strHelpID;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_strHelpID != value)
				{
					_strHelpID = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("HelpID");
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<SplitTreeNode> TreeNodeList => _treeNodeList;

		public override bool CanPrint
		{
			get
			{
				if (explorerTree != null && explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode && (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_pure || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_azeotrope || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_Singular || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_report))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanPrintPreview
		{
			get
			{
				if (explorerTree != null && explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode && (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_pure || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_azeotrope || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_Singular))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public AzeotropeAnalysisScreen(IUnityContainer container)
		{
			if (container == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("container");
			}
			Container = container;
			InitializeComponent();
			explorerTree.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(explorerTree_MouseLeftBtnDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			((FrameworkElement)this).Loaded += AzeotropeAnalysisWindow_Loaded;
		}

		private void AzeotropeAnalysisWindow_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext = this;
				pageAzeoInput.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageAzeoInput.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pagePureComponent.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pagePureComponent.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageAzeoComposition.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageAzeoComposition.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageSingular.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageSingular.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageAzeoReport.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageAzeoReport.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_sTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
				pageAzeoInput.m_objAzeotropeSearch = m_objAzeotropeSearch;
				pagePureComponent.m_objDataClass = m_objAzeotropeSearch;
				pageAzeoComposition.m_objDataClass = m_objAzeotropeSearch;
				pageSingular.m_objDataClass = m_objAzeotropeSearch;
				pageAzeoReport.m_objDataClass = m_objAzeotropeSearch;
				IHapp happ = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<object>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, "RootApplicationObject", Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as IHapp;
				IHNode rootNode = null;
				if (happ != null)
				{
					rootNode = happ.Tree;
				}
				Init(rootNode, null);
				UpdateExplorerTree();
				ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
				HelpID = "1";
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		public void Init(IHNode rootNode, DistillationApp engine, int lParam = 0, string strParam = "")
		{
			try
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode = rootNode;
				AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenEngine = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Application as IAPPDF;
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ComponentNode = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Elements["Data", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Components", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Specifications", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Input", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing];
				AzeoGUIGlobals.PropertyNode = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Elements["Data", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Properties", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Specifications", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Input", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing];
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			if (engine == null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).Cursor = Cursors.Wait;
				engine = AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp;
				((FrameworkElement)this).Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
				AzeoGUIGlobals.g_WindowsCount = 0;
			}
			else
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp = engine;
			}
			try
			{
				m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_IAzeotropeSearch = AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp.GetAzeotropeSearch();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetAzeotropeSearchFailed);
				StopControl();
			}
			LoadPropertyData();
			AzeoGUIGlobals.g_WindowsCount++;
		}

		public void LoadPropertyData()
		{
			pageAzeoInput.UpdateFormControl();
		}

		private void StopControl()
		{
			IUnityContainer unityContainer = ((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer;
			((unityContainer != null) ? UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellApplication>(unityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) : null).ActiveWindow.CloseScreen((ScreenContentControl)(object)this);
		}

		public void UpdateExplorerTree()
		{
			TreeNodeList.Clear();
			SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode = new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.AzeotropeAnalysis,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "inputfolder2.png"
			};
			TreeNodeList.Add(splitTreeNode);
			SplitTreeNode item = new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "resultsfolder1.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_input
			};
			splitTreeNode.Nodes.Add(item);
			SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode2 = new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Output,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "resultsfolder2.png"
			};
			splitTreeNode.Nodes.Add(splitTreeNode2);
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.PureComponents,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "good.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_pure
			});
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Azeotropes,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "good.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_azeotrope
			});
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SingularPoints,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "good.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_Singular
			});
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Report,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "report.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_report
			});
			explorerTree.DataContext = TreeNodeList;
		}

		private void ShowForm(TernaryControl formID)
		{
			switch (formID)
			{
			case TernaryControl.t_input:
				HelpID = "2";
				pageAzeoInput.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_pure:
				HelpID = "22";
				if (!pageAzeoInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					UpdateExplorerTree();
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageAzeoInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.ConfigureGrid(GridType.GT_Pure);
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_azeotrope:
				HelpID = "23";
				if (!pageAzeoInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					UpdateExplorerTree();
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageAzeoInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.ConfigureGrid(GridType.GT_Azeo);
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_Singular:
				HelpID = "24";
				if (!pageAzeoInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					UpdateExplorerTree();
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageAzeoInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.ConfigureGrid(GridType.GT_Both);
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_report:
				HelpID = "25";
				if (!pageAzeoInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					UpdateExplorerTree();
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageAzeoInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageAzeoReport.GenerateReport();
				break;
			default:
				HelpID = "1";
				break;
			}
		}

		private void explorerTree_MouseLeftBtnDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode)
			{
				ShowForm(splitTreeNode.FormID);
				if (string.Compare(splitTreeNode.Text, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Output) == 0)
				{
					HelpID = "22";
				}
			}
		}

		private void page_UpdateStatusBar(object source, UpdateStatusEventArgs e)
		{
			string text = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Status)) ? e.Status : WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Idle);
			txtStatus.Text = text;
		}

		private void page_InProgress(object source, InPropgressEventArgs e)
		{
			ProgressIndicator(e.IsRunning);
		}

		private void ProgressIndicator(bool p)
		{
		}

		public override bool DoCustomPrint()
		{
			if (explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode { FormID: TernaryControl.t_report } && pageAzeoReport.webBrowser1.Document is IHTMLDocument2 iHTMLDocument)
			{
				iHTMLDocument.execCommand("Print", showUI: true, Type.Missing);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> GeneratePrintPages(System.Windows.Size availablePrintSize)
		{
			if (explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode)
			{
				if (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_pure)
				{
					return GenericScrollAreaPrinter.GenerateGenericPrintPages(pagePureComponent.igGrid, availablePrintSize, 0, 1, "Split Azeotrope Analysis - Pure Components", (string)null, false, (Func<Field, string>)null);
				}
				if (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_azeotrope)
				{
					return GenericScrollAreaPrinter.GenerateGenericPrintPages(pageAzeoComposition.igGrid, availablePrintSize, 0, 1, "Split Azeotrope Analysis - Azeotropes", (string)null, false, (Func<Field, string>)null);
				}
				if (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_Singular)
				{
					return GenericScrollAreaPrinter.GenerateGenericPrintPages(pageSingular.igGrid, availablePrintSize, 0, 1, "Split Azeotrope Analysis - Singular Points", (string)null, false, (Func<Field, string>)null);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void ButtonInContextGuidance_ContextIdRequested(object sender, ContextIdRequestedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0061: Expected O, but got Unknown
			UIElement uIElement = theContainerGrid.Children.Cast<UIElement>().FirstOrDefault((UIElement x) => x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible);
			if (uIElement != null)
			{
				e.ContextId = AppLevelControlSettings.Default.InContextGuidanceContextIdGenerator?.Invoke(new InContextGuidanceContextIdGenerationArgs(MMHelp.GetHelpId((DependencyObject)uIElement), MMHelp.GetHelpFile((DependencyObject)uIElement), (object)null));
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			//IL_009f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a9: Expected O, but got Unknown
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				explorerTree = (TreeView)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				rightViewPanel = (UserControl)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				theContainerGrid = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				pageAzeoInput = (UsrAzeoInput)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				pagePureComponent = (UserCompositionGrid)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				pageAzeoComposition = (UserCompositionGrid)target;
				break;
			case 7:
				pageSingular = (UserCompositionGrid)target;
				break;
			case 8:
				pageAzeoReport = (UsrAzeoReport)target;
				break;
			case 9:
				buttonInContextGuidance = (MMInContextGuidanceButton)target;
				buttonInContextGuidance.ContextIdRequested += ButtonInContextGuidance_ContextIdRequested;
				break;
			case 10:
				txtStatus = (TextBlock)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

	public class PressureChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{

		public PressureChangedEventArgs(double pressure)

			Pressure = pressure;
		}

		}

	public class SplitTreeNode : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private ImageSource _icon;

		private string _iconName;

		private ObservableCollection<SplitTreeNode> _nodes = new ObservableCollection<SplitTreeNode>();

		private bool _isSelected;

		public string Text { get; set; }

		public ImageSource Icon
		{
			get
			{
				return _icon;
			}
			set
			{
				_icon = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("Icon");
			}
		}

		public string IconName
		{
			get
			{
				return _iconName;
			}
			set
			{
				_iconName = value;
				Icon = ImageResources.GetImageSource(_iconName);
			}
		}

		public string Tag { get; set; }

		public TernaryControl FormID { get; set; }

		public ObservableCollection<SplitTreeNode> Nodes => _nodes;

		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				return _isSelected;
			}
			set
			{
				_isSelected = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsSelected");
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}
	}
	public class TernaryMapScreen : ApwnDocumentControl, INotifyPropertyChanged, IComponentConnector
	{
		private ImmiscibilityEnum _lastSelectImmiscibility = ImmiscibilityEnum.None;

		private bool _hasLoaded;

		private double _pressure;

		private ObservableCollection<string> _pressureUnitsList = new ObservableCollection<string>();

		private string _pressureUnit;

		private bool[] _CheckedRecord = new bool[8];

		private bool _isVLLEnvChecked;

		private bool _isLLEnvChecked;

		private bool _isTieLinesChecked;

		public double Pressure
		{
			get
			{
				return _pressure;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_pressure != value && value > 0.0)
				{
					_pressure = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Pressure");
					m_objDistillationMap.m_bInputChanged = true;
					m_objDistillationMap.m_PressureChanged = true;
					pageTernayInput.Pressure = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> PressureUnitsList => _pressureUnitsList;

		public string PressureUnit
		{
			get
			{
				return _pressureUnit;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_pressureUnit != value)
				{
					_pressureUnit = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("PressureUnit");
					pageTernayInput.PresUnit = value;
					m_objDistillationMap.m_bInputChanged = true;
					m_objDistillationMap.m_PressureChanged = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsVLLEnvChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isVLLEnvChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isVLLEnvChecked == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isVLLEnvChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsVLLEnvChecked");
				chkLL.IsEnabled = !_isVLLEnvChecked;
				UpdateView(chkView.CV_VLLE, value);
				chkTieLines.IsEnabled = value;
				chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = value;
				if (pageTernayInput.IsVLLEnvelopeChecked != value)
				{
					pageTernayInput.IsVLLEnvelopeChecked = value;
				}
				if (value)
				{
					if (_lastSelectImmiscibility != ImmiscibilityEnum.VLLEnvelope && PlaitTemp == 0.0)
					{
						RefreshPlotWithInputChanged();
					}
					_lastSelectImmiscibility = ImmiscibilityEnum.VLLEnvelope;
					IsTieLinesChecked = _CheckedRecord[4];
				}
				else
				{
					_CheckedRecord[4] = IsTieLinesChecked;
					IsTieLinesChecked = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsLLEnvChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isLLEnvChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isLLEnvChecked == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isLLEnvChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsLLEnvChecked");
				if (pageTernayInput.IsLLEEnvelopeChecked != value)
				{
					pageTernayInput.IsLLEEnvelopeChecked = value;
				}
				chkVLL.IsEnabled = !value;
				UpdateView(chkView.CV_LLE, value);
				chkTieLines.IsEnabled = value;
				if (value)
				{
					if (_lastSelectImmiscibility != ImmiscibilityEnum.LLEnvelope)
					{
						RefreshPlotWithInputChanged();
					}
					_lastSelectImmiscibility = ImmiscibilityEnum.LLEnvelope;
					IsTieLinesChecked = _CheckedRecord[4];
				}
				else
				{
					chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = false;
					_CheckedRecord[4] = IsTieLinesChecked;
					IsTieLinesChecked = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsTieLinesChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isTieLinesChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isTieLinesChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsTieLinesChecked");
				if (m_objDistillationMap.m_CalcThis[10])
				{
					chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = value;
					if (value)
					{
						IsVaporCurveChecked = true;
					}
				}
				UpdateViewTieLinesToolBar(value);
				UpdateViewTieLines(0, value && IsVLLEnvChecked);
				UpdateViewTieLines(1, value && IsLLEnvChecked);
			}
		}

		public double LLE
		{
			get
			{
				return _LLE;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_LLE != value)
				{
					_LLE = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("LLE");
					pageTernayInput.LLTemp = value;
					m_objDistillationMap.m_bInputChanged = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> LLEUnitsList => _LLEUnitsList;

		public string LLEUnit

				}
			}
		}

		public double PlaitTemp
		{
			get
			{
				return _PlaitTemp;
			}
			set
			{
				_PlaitTemp = value;
				if (value == 0.0)
				{
					txtPlaitTemp.Text = "";
					return;
				}
				double num = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTempDouble(value, PlaitTempUnit);
				txtPlaitTemp.Text = $"{num:0.00}";
				cboPlaitTempUnits.Text = pageTernayInput.SelectedTempUnits;
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> PlaitTempUnitsList => _PlaitTempUnitsList;

		public string PlaitTempUnit
		{
			get
			{
				return _PlaitTempUnit;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_PlaitTempUnit != value)
				{
					_PlaitTempUnit = value;
					pageTernayInput.PlaitTempUnit = value;
					if (PlaitTemp > 0.0)
					{
						double num = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTempDouble(PlaitTemp, PlaitTempUnit);
						txtPlaitTemp.Text = $"{num:0.00}";
					}
					else
					{
						txtPlaitTemp.Text = "";
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsAzeotropesChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isAzeotropesChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isAzeotropesChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsAzeotropesChecked");
				pageTernaryPlot.FinishedAdding();
				IsBPTempChecked = value;
				UpdateView(chkView.CV_Azeotropes, value);
			}
		}

		public override bool CanPrint
		{
			get
			{
				if (explorerTree != null && explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode && (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_pure || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_azeotrope || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_Singular || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_report))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public override bool CanPrintPreview
		{
			get
			{
				if (explorerTree != null && explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode && (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_pure || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_azeotrope || splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_Singular))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public TernaryMapScreen()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			((FrameworkElement)this).Loaded += TernaryMapScreen_Loaded;
		}

		private void TernaryMapScreen_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext = this;
				pageTernayInput.PressureChanged += pageTernayInput_PressureChanged;
				pageTernayInput.PressureUnitsChanged += pageTernayInput_PressureUnitsChanged;
				pageTernayInput.LLTempChanged += pageTernayInput_LLTempChanged;
				pageTernayInput.TempUnitsChanged += pageTernayInput_TempUnitsChanged;
				pageTernayInput.CheckCurves += pageTernayInput_CheckCurves;
				pageTernayInput.CheckAzeotropes += pageTernayInput_CheckAzeotropes;
				pageTernayInput.CheckDistillBound += pageTernayInput_CheckDistillBound;
				pageTernayInput.CheckIsovolat += pageTernayInput_CheckIsovolat;
				pageTernayInput.CheckLLE += pageTernayInput_CheckLLE;
				pageTernayInput.CheckVLLE += pageTernayInput_CheckVLLE;
				pageTernayInput.StopCurveMenu += pageTernayInput_StopCurveMenu;
				pageTernayInput.NoEnvelopes += pageTernayInput_NoEnvelopes;
				pageTernayInput.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pagePureComponent.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pagePureComponent.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageSingular.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageSingular.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageAzeoComposition.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageAzeoComposition.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageAzeoReport.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageAzeoReport.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageTernaryPlot.UpdateStatusBar += page_UpdateStatusBar;
				pageTernaryPlot.InProgress += page_InProgress;
				pageTernayInput.DistillationMapObj = m_objDistillationMap;
				pagePureComponent.m_objDataClass = m_objDistillationMap;
				pageSingular.m_objDataClass = m_objDistillationMap;
				pageAzeoComposition.m_objDataClass = m_objDistillationMap;
				pageAzeoReport.m_objDataClass = m_objDistillationMap;
				pageTernaryPlot.DistillationMapObj = m_objDistillationMap;
				IsAzeotropesChecked = true;
				chkAzeotropes.IsEnabled = false;
				IsDistBoudChecked = true;
				chkDistilBound.IsEnabled = false;
				IsDisCurveChecked = true;
				chkResDisCurves.IsEnabled = false;
				IsIsovoChecked = true;
				chkIsovolatility.IsEnabled = false;
				IsTieLinesChecked = true;
				IsVaporCurveChecked = true;
				IsBPTempChecked = true;
				IsMarkersChecked = true;
				IHapp happ = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<object>(((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer, "RootApplicationObject", Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as IHapp;
				IHNode rootNode = null;
				if (happ != null)
				{
					rootNode = happ.Tree;
				}
				Init(rootNode, null);
				LLEUnitsList.Clear();
				string[] gTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits;
				foreach (string item in gTempUnits)
				{
					LLEUnitsList.Add(item);
				}
				LLEUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
				if (double.TryParse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempValue, out var result))
				{
					LLE = result;
				}
				LLEUnitsList.Clear();
				gTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits;
				foreach (string item2 in gTempUnits)
				{
					LLEUnitsList.Add(item2);
				}
				LLEUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
				if (double.TryParse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempValue, out result))
				{
					LLE = result;
				}
				PlaitTempUnitsList.Clear();
				gTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits;
				foreach (string item3 in gTempUnits)
				{
					PlaitTempUnitsList.Add(item3);
				}
				PlaitTempUnit = pageTernayInput.SelectedTempUnits;
				PressureUnitsList.Clear();
				gTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureUnits;
				foreach (string item4 in gTempUnits)
				{
					PressureUnitsList.Add(item4);
				}
				PressureUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureDef];
				if (double.TryParse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureValue, out result))
				{
					Pressure = result;
				}
				UpdateExplorerTree();
				ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
				HelpID = "26";
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
		}

		private void RefreshPlotWithInputChanged()
		{
			try
			{
				if (pageTernaryPlot.HasLoaded() && m_objDistillationMap.m_bInputChanged && pageTernayInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					pageTernaryPlot.PlotMap();
					if (IsVLLEnvChecked)
					{
						pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp = m_objDistillationMap.m_dbPlaitTemp;
					}
					else
					{
						pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp = 0.0;
					}
					PlaitTemp = pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message + " " + ex.StackTrace);
			}
		}

		private void Init(IHNode rootNode, DistillationApp engine, int lParam = 0, string strParam = "")
		{
			try
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode = rootNode;
				AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenEngine = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Application as IAPPDF;
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ComponentNode = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Elements["Data", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Components", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Specifications", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Input", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing];
				AzeoGUIGlobals.PropertyNode = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Elements["Data", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Properties", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Specifications", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Input", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing];
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			AzeoGUIGlobals.ResetFlags();
			if (engine == null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).Cursor = Cursors.Wait;
				engine = AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp;
				((FrameworkElement)this).Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
				AzeoGUIGlobals.g_WindowsCount = 0;
			}
			else
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp = engine;
			}
			try
			{
				m_objDistillationMap.m_IDistillationMap = AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp.GetDistillationMap();
				for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i++)
				{
					m_objDistillationMap.m_ReCalcThis[i] = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetDistillationMapFailed);
				StopControl();
			}
			LoadPropertyData();
			AzeoGUIGlobals.g_WindowsCount++;
			m_objDistillationMap.m_PlottedBefore = false;
			m_objDistillationMap.m_ReCalcTernary = true;
			m_objDistillationMap.m_TernaryMapInit = true;
			m_objDistillationMap.m_ReCalcEverything = true;
		}

		private void LoadPropertyData()
		{
			pageTernayInput.UpdateFormControl();
		}

		private void StopControl()
		{
			IUnityContainer unityContainer = ((ScreenContentControl)this).UnityContainer;
			((unityContainer != null) ? UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellApplication>(unityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) : null).ActiveWindow.CloseScreen((ScreenContentControl)(object)this);
		}

		private void UpdateViewTieLinesToolBar(bool status)
		{
			pageTernaryPlot.SetToolbar("TieLine", status);
			pageTernaryPlot.SetToolbar("TieLineValue", status);
		}

		private void UpdateViewMarkersToolBar(bool status)
		{
			pageTernaryPlot.SetToolbar("Marker", status);
			pageTernaryPlot.SetToolbar("MarkerValue", status);
		}

		private void UpdateViewTieLines(int index, bool status)
		{
			switch (index)
			{
			case 0:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
				break;
			case 1:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineLLE);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateView(chkView index, bool status)
		{
			switch (index)
			{
			case chkView.CV_LLE:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.EnvLLE);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_VLLE:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.EnvVLLE);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Azeotropes:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.MarkAzeotrope);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_DisBound:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_TieLines:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineLLE);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_ResDisCurve:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.CurveResidue);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.CurveDistill);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_IsovolCurve:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.CurveIso);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Markers:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.MarkTieLine);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.MarkDistillCurve);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.MarkUserPoint);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TextUser);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_VaporLine:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.MarkVLLE);
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Temperature:
				pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(status, ObjType.TextAzeotrope);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_ResidueOnly:
				break;
			}
		}

		private void AzeoBlock(bool status)
		{
			if (TreeNodeList.Count >= 1)
			{
				string iconName = (status ? "good.png" : "bad1.png");
				SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode = FindTreeNode(TreeNodeList[0], TernaryControl.t_azeotrope);
				if (splitTreeNode != null)
				{
					splitTreeNode.IconName = iconName;
				}
				SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode2 = FindTreeNode(TreeNodeList[0], TernaryControl.t_Singular);
				if (splitTreeNode2 != null)
				{
					splitTreeNode2.IconName = iconName;
				}
			}
		}

		private SplitTreeNode FindTreeNode(SplitTreeNode parentNode, TernaryControl iForm)
		{
			foreach (SplitTreeNode node in parentNode.Nodes)
			{
				if (node.FormID == iForm)
				{
					return node;
				}
				SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode = FindTreeNode(node, iForm);
				if (splitTreeNode != null)
				{
					return splitTreeNode;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void UpdateExplorerTree()
		{
			TreeNodeList.Clear();
			SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode = new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.TernaryMapAnalysis,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "inputfolder2.png"
			};
			TreeNodeList.Add(splitTreeNode);
			SplitTreeNode item = new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "resultsfolder1.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_input
			};
			splitTreeNode.Nodes.Add(item);
			SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode2 = new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Output,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "resultsfolder2.png"
			};
			splitTreeNode.Nodes.Add(splitTreeNode2);
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.PureComponents,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "good.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_pure
			});
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Azeotropes,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "good.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_azeotrope
			});
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SingularPoints,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "good.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_Singular
			});
			splitTreeNode2.Nodes.Add(new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Report,
				Tag = "good",
				IconName = "report.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_report
			});
			SplitTreeNode item2 = new SplitTreeNode
			{
				Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.TernaryPlot,
				Tag = "plot",
				IconName = "ternaryfolder1.png",
				FormID = TernaryControl.t_plot
			};
			splitTreeNode.Nodes.Add(item2);
			explorerTree.DataContext = TreeNodeList;
		}

		private void SetSelectedNode(TernaryControl formID)
		{
			SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode = FindTreeNode(TreeNodeList[0], formID);
			if (splitTreeNode != null && !splitTreeNode.IsSelected)
			{
				splitTreeNode.IsSelected = true;
			}
		}

		private void leftTabCtrl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (leftTabCtrl.SelectedIndex == 1)
			{
				ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_plot);
			}
		}

		private void explorerTree_SelectedItemChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<object> e)
		{
			if (explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode)
			{
				ShowForm(splitTreeNode.FormID);
				if (string.Compare(splitTreeNode.Text, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Output) == 0)
				{
					HelpID = "22";
				}
			}
		}

		private void page_UpdateStatusBar(object source, UpdateStatusEventArgs e)
		{
			string txt;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Status))
			{
				txt = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Idle;
			}
			else
			{
				txt = e.Status;
			}
			((DispatcherObject)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				txtStatus.Text = txt;
			}, null);
		}

		}

		private void SetEnvelopes(bool stat)
		{
			chkVLL.IsEnabled = stat;
			chkLL.IsEnabled = stat;
			IsVaporCurveChecked = stat;
			chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = stat;
			IsTieLinesChecked = stat;
			chkTieLines.IsEnabled = stat;
		}

		private void pageTernayInput_NoEnvelopes(object source, NoEnvelopesEventArgs e)
		{
			SetEnvelopes(!e.Stat);
			pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(!e.Stat, ObjType.EnvLLE);
			pageTernaryPlot.DisplayItem(!e.Stat, ObjType.EnvVLLE);
		}

		}

		private void pageTernayInput_CheckLLE(object source, CheckLLEEventArgs e)
		{
			IsVLLEnvChecked = e.Pos;
			chkVLL.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
			chkLL.IsEnabled = !e.Pos;
			IsTieLinesChecked = e.Pos;
			chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = !e.Pos;
			IsVaporCurveChecked = e.Pos;
			chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
			IsTieLinesChecked = e.Pos;
			chkTieLines.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
		}

		private void pageTernayInput_CheckVLLE(object source, CheckVLLEventArgs e)
		{
			chkVLL.IsEnabled = !e.Pos;
			chkLL.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
			IsLLEnvChecked = e.Pos;
			IsTieLinesChecked = e.Pos;
			chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
			IsVaporCurveChecked = !e.Pos;
			chkVaporCurve.IsEnabled = !e.Pos;
		}

		private void pageTernayInput_CheckIsovolat(object source, CheckIsovolatEventArgs e)
		{
			IsIsovoChecked = e.Pos;
			chkIsovolatility.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
		}

		private void pageTernayInput_CheckDistillBound(object source, CheckDistillBoundEventArgs e)
		{
			IsDistBoudChecked = e.Pos;
			chkDistilBound.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
		}

		private void pageTernayInput_CheckAzeotropes(object source, CheckAzeotropesEventArgs e)
		{
			IsAzeotropesChecked = e.Pos;
			chkAzeotropes.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
			AzeoBlock(e.Pos);
			IsDistBoudChecked = e.Pos;
			chkDistilBound.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
		}

		private void pageTernayInput_CheckCurves(object source, CheckCurvesEventArgs e)

			chkResDisCurves.IsEnabled = e.Pos;
		}

		}

		private void ShowForm(TernaryControl formID)
		{
			switch (formID)
			{
			case TernaryControl.t_input:
				HelpID = "27";
				pageTernayInput.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageTernaryPlot.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				((DispatcherObject)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					SetSelectedNode(TernaryControl.t_input);
				});
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_pure:
				HelpID = "28";
				if (!pageTernayInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageTernayInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageTernaryPlot.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				if (!pagePureComponent.ConfigureGrid(GridType.GT_Pure))
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
				}
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_azeotrope:
				HelpID = "29";
				if (!pageTernayInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageTernayInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageTernaryPlot.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				if (!pageAzeoComposition.ConfigureGrid(GridType.GT_Azeo))
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
				}
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_Singular:
				HelpID = "30";
				if (!pageTernayInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageTernayInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageTernaryPlot.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				if (!pageSingular.ConfigureGrid(GridType.GT_Both))
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
				}
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_report:
				HelpID = "31";
				if (!pageTernayInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				pageTernayInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageTernaryPlot.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				pageAzeoReport.GenerateReport();
				break;
			case TernaryControl.t_plot:
			{
				HelpID = "32";
				if (!pageTernayInput.Validate_Input())
				{
					ShowForm(TernaryControl.t_input);
					break;
				}
				int num = 1;
				if (m_objDistillationMap.m_bInputChanged)
				{
					num = pageTernaryPlot.PlotMap();
					if (IsVLLEnvChecked)
					{
						pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp = m_objDistillationMap.m_dbPlaitTemp;
					}
					else
					{
						pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp = 0.0;
					}
					PlaitTemp = pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp;
				}
				switch (num)
				{
				case -1:
					StopControl();
					break;
				case 0:
					break;
				default:
					pageTernayInput.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					pagePureComponent.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					pageAzeoComposition.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					pageSingular.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					pageTernaryPlot.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					pageAzeoReport.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					((DispatcherObject)this).Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
					{
						tabPlotInput.IsSelected = true;
					}, null);
					SetSelectedNode(formID);
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
			default:
				HelpID = "26";
				break;
			}
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		public override bool DoCustomPrint()
		{
			if (explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode { FormID: TernaryControl.t_report } && pageAzeoReport.webBrowser1.Document is IHTMLDocument2 iHTMLDocument)
			{
				iHTMLDocument.execCommand("Print", showUI: true, Type.Missing);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> GeneratePrintPages(System.Windows.Size availablePrintSize)
		{
			if (explorerTree.SelectedValue is SplitTreeNode splitTreeNode)
			{
				if (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_pure)
				{
					return GenericScrollAreaPrinter.GenerateGenericPrintPages(pagePureComponent.igGrid, availablePrintSize, 0, 1, "Ternary Maps Analysis - Pure Components", (string)null, false, (Func<Field, string>)null);
				}
				if (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_azeotrope)
				{
					return GenericScrollAreaPrinter.GenerateGenericPrintPages(pageAzeoComposition.igGrid, availablePrintSize, 0, 1, "Ternary Maps Analysis - Azeotropes", (string)null, false, (Func<Field, string>)null);
				}
				if (splitTreeNode.FormID == TernaryControl.t_Singular)
				{
					return GenericScrollAreaPrinter.GenerateGenericPrintPages(pageSingular.igGrid, availablePrintSize, 0, 1, "Ternary Maps Analysis - Singular Points", (string)null, false, (Func<Field, string>)null);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void txtPressure_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return && double.TryParse(txtPressure.Text.Trim(), out var result) && result != Pressure)
			{
				Pressure = result;
				m_objDistillationMap.m_bInputChanged = true;
				m_objDistillationMap.m_PressureChanged = true;
				pageTernayInput.Pressure = result;
				if (pageTernaryPlot.PlotMap() == -1)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.PropertySystemError);
				}
				if (IsVLLEnvChecked)
				{
					pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp = m_objDistillationMap.m_dbPlaitTemp;
				}
				else
				{
					pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp = 0.0;
				}
				PlaitTemp = pageTernayInput.PlaitTemp;
				UpdateView(chkView.CV_Temperature, IsBPTempChecked);
			}
		}

		private void txtLLE_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return && double.TryParse(txtLLE.Text.Trim(), out var result) && result != LLE)
			{
				LLE = result;
				m_objDistillationMap.m_bInputChanged = true;
				pageTernayInput.LLTemp = result;
				if (pageTernaryPlot.PlotMap() == -1)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.PropertySystemError);
				}
				UpdateView(chkView.CV_Temperature, IsBPTempChecked);
			}
		}

		private void txtPressure_PreviewTextInput(object sender, TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = txtPressure.SelectionStart;
			if (e.Text[0] == '-')
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!double.TryParse((txtPressure.Text + e.Text).Trim(), out var result) || result < 0.0)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			((UIElement)this).OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}

		private void txtLLE_PeviewTextInput(object sender, TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = txtLLE.SelectionStart;
			if (e.Text[0] == '-')
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!double.TryParse((txtLLE.Text + e.Text).Trim(), out var result) || result < 0.0)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			((UIElement)this).OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}

		private void ButtonInContextGuidance_ContextIdRequested(object sender, ContextIdRequestedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0061: Expected O, but got Unknown
			UIElement uIElement = theContainerGrid.Children.Cast<UIElement>().FirstOrDefault((UIElement x) => x.Visibility == Visibility.Visible);
			if (uIElement != null)
			{
				e.ContextId = AppLevelControlSettings.Default.InContextGuidanceContextIdGenerator?.Invoke(new InContextGuidanceContextIdGenerationArgs(MMHelp.GetHelpId((DependencyObject)uIElement), MMHelp.GetHelpFile((DependencyObject)uIElement), (object)null));
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			//IL_02b0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02ba: Expected O, but got Unknown
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				leftTabCtrl = (TabControl)target;
				leftTabCtrl.SelectionChanged += leftTabCtrl_SelectionChanged;
				break;
			case 2:
				tabExplorer = (TabItem)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				explorerTree = (TreeView)target;
				explorerTree.SelectedItemChanged += explorerTree_SelectedItemChanged;
				break;
			case 4:
				tabPlotInput = (TabItem)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				grpPressure = (GroupBox)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				txtPressure = (TextBox)target;
				txtPressure.PreviewTextInput += txtPressure_PreviewTextInput;
				txtPressure.KeyUp += txtPressure_KeyUp;
				break;
			case 7:
				cboPressureUnit = (ComboBox)target;
				break;
			case 8:
				grpImmiscibility = (GroupBox)target;
				break;
			case 9:

				break;
			case 10:
				chkLL = (CheckBox)target;
				break;
			case 11:
				txtLLE = (TextBox)target;
				txtLLE.PreviewTextInput += txtLLE_PeviewTextInput;
				txtLLE.KeyUp += txtLLE_KeyUp;
				break;
			case 12:
				cboLLEUnits = (ComboBox)target;
				break;

				break;
			case 14:
				chkAzeotropes = (CheckBox)target;
				break;

				break;
