		public TriPlot()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_pfsplot1 = new AxPfsplot();
			plotHost.Child = _pfsplot1;
			_pfsplot1.CreateControl();
			plotHost.PreviewKeyDown += delegate(object o, KeyEventArgs e)
			{
				if (e.Key == Key.Up || e.Key == Key.Down || e.Key == Key.Left || e.Key == Key.Right || e.Key == Key.Apps)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
					int nChar = 0;
					switch (e.Key)
					{
					case Key.Left:
						nChar = 37;
						break;
					case Key.Up:
						nChar = 38;
						break;
					case Key.Right:
						nChar = 39;
						break;
					case Key.Down:
						nChar = 40;
						break;
					case Key.Apps:
						nChar = 93;
						break;
					}
					_pfsplot1.KeyDown(nChar, 1, 0);
				}
			};
			base.Loaded += TriPlot_Loaded;
			base.Unloaded += TriPlot_Unloaded;
			_pfsplot1.KeyUpEvent += _pfsplot1_KeyUpEvent;
			_pfsplot1.PointerExit += _pfsplot1_PointerExit;
			_pfsplot1.Scrolled += _pfsplot1_Scrolled;
			_pfsplot1.PointerLocation += _pfsplot1_PointerLocation;
			_pfsplot1.OnClick += _pfsplot1_OnClick;
			_pfsplot1.RegionDefined += _pfsplot1_RegionDefined;
			_pfsplot1.OnRBClick += _pfsplot1_OnRBClick;
		}

		private void TriPlot_Unloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\AspenTech");
			if (registryKey == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				registryKey.CreateSubKey($"Aspen Split\\{ASplitVersion.ASPENSPLIT_VERSION_STRING}\\Distillation Analysis");
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return;
			}
			registryKey.Close();
			registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey($"Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Split\\{ASplitVersion.ASPENSPLIT_VERSION_STRING}\\Distillation Analysis");
			if (registryKey == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				registryKey.SetValue("COORDINATES", CoDisplay, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("TOOLBARPOS", ToolBarPos, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("TRANSPARENTLABELS", TransparentLabels, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("BOILINGPOINT", CalcBoilPoint, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("FONTNAME", Properties.PFont.PName, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("FONTSIZE", CoDisplay, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("RESIDUECOLOUR", Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("RESIDUELINE", Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("RESIDUEMARKER", Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("RESIDUEMARKSIZE", Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("DISTILLCOLOUR", Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("DISTILLLINE", Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("DISTILLMARKER", Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("DISTILLMARKSIZE", Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("AZEOTROPECOLOUR", Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("AZEOTROPEMARKER", Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("AZEOTROPEMARKSIZE", Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("USERMARKCOLOUR", Properties.PUserMarker.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("USERMARKMARKER", Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("USERMARKMARKSIZE", Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("TIELINECOLOUR", Properties.PTieLine.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("TIELINELINE", Properties.PTieLine.PLine, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("TIELINEMARKER", Properties.PTieLine.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("TIELINEMARKSIZE", Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("TIELINEMARKER2", Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("USERLINECOLOUR", Properties.PUserLine.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("USERLINELINE", Properties.PUserLine.PLine, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("USERLINEMARKER", Properties.PUserLine.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("USERLINEMARKSIZE", Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("ENVELOPECOLOUR", Properties.PEnvelope.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("ENVELOPEPATTERN", Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("BOUNDRYCOLOUR", Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("BOUNDRYMARKER", Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("BOUNDRYMARKSIZE", Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("ISOCURVECOLOUR", Properties.PIsoCurve.PColour, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("ISOCURVEMARKER", Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarker, RegistryValueKind.String);
				registryKey.SetValue("ISOCURVEMARKSIZE", Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarkerSize, RegistryValueKind.String);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey.Close();
			}
		}

		private void TriPlot_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_hasLoaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			_pfsplot1.DisplayType = 2;
			DefaultProperties();
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey($"Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Split\\{ASplitVersion.ASPENSPLIT_VERSION_STRING}\\Distillation Analysis");
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				try
				{
					CoDisplay = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("COORDINATES"));
					ToolBarPos = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("TOOLBARPOS"));
					TransparentLabels = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("TRANSPARENTLABELS"));
					CalcBoilPoint = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("BOILINGPOINT"));
					Properties.PFont.PName = registryKey.GetValue("FONTNAME").ToString();
					Properties.PFont.PSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("FONTSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPLine>(registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUELINE").ToString(), out Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUEMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker);
					Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUEMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLCOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPLine>(registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLLINE").ToString(), out Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker);
					Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("AZEOTROPECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("AZEOTROPEMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker);
					Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("AZEOTROPEMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("USERMARKCOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PUserMarker.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("USERMARKMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker);
					Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("USERMARKMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPLine>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINELINE").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PLine);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINEMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PMarker);
					Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINEMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINEMARKER2").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2);
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("USERLINECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PUserLine.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPLine>(registryKey.GetValue("USERLINELINE").ToString(), out Properties.PUserLine.PLine);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("USERLINEMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PUserLine.PMarker);
					Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("USERLINEMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("ENVELOPECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PEnvelope.PColour);
					Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("ENVELOPEPATTERN"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("BOUNDRYCOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("BOUNDRYMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker);
					Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("BOUNDRYMARKSIZE"));
					Properties.PDistBoundry.PLine = TPLine.Thick1;
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("ISOCURVECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PIsoCurve.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("ISOCURVEMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarker);
					Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("ISOCURVEMARKSIZE"));
					Properties.PIsoCurve.PLine = TPLine.DottedDash;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					registryKey.Close();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DefaultProperties();
			}
			AddCurveOption = true;
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(1, onOff: true, @default: true);
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(2, onOff: true, @default: true);
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(3, onOff: true, @default: false);
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(4, onOff: true, @default: false);
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(7, onOff: true, @default: false);
			_pfsplot1.SetCopyMode(1);
			_pfsplot1.SetHighlightMode(1);
			((base.Resources["mnuMarker"] as ContextMenu).Items[3] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			ContextMenu contextMenu = base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu;
			((contextMenu.Items[8] as MenuItem).Items[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < WhatsShowing.Length; i++)
			{
				WhatsShowing[i] = true;
			}
			Clear();
			MenuItem menuItem = contextMenu.Items[9] as MenuItem;
			if (PlotType == PlotType.Equilateral)
			{
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SwitchToRightTriangle;
			}
			else
			{
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SwitchToEnquilateral;
			}
			(contextMenu.Items[3] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			(contextMenu.Items[4] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			(contextMenu.Items[5] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			RefreshComponents();
			codisp.Text = "-.--" + Environment.NewLine + "-.--" + Environment.NewLine + "-.--";
			if (TPBasis == AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mass)
			{
				lBasis.Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassBasis;
			}
			else
			{
				lBasis.Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleBasis;
			}
			_hasLoaded = true;
		}

		private void _pfsplot1_KeyUpEvent(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_KeyUpEvent e)
		{
			if ((e.keyCode == 46 || e.keyCode == 8) && DeleteItem(LastID))
			{
				LastID.Id = -1;
			}
			this.KeyUp(this, new KeyUpEventArgs(e.keyCode));
		}

		private void _pfsplot1_PointerExit(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			codisp.Text = "-.--" + Environment.NewLine + "-.--" + Environment.NewLine + "-.--";
		}

		private void _pfsplot1_Scrolled(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_ScrolledEvent e)
		{
		}

		private void _pfsplot1_PointerLocation(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_PointerLocationEvent e)
		{
			if (UserMode == DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLineEnd)
			{
				_pfsplot1.DragLine(LineStart.X, LineStart.Y, e.ix, e.iy, -1);
			}
			string[] array = e.coordinate.Split('\n');
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				array[i] = array[i].Substring(2, array[i].Length - 2).Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol());
			}
			float num = float.Parse(array[0], NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			float num2 = float.Parse(array[1], NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			float num3 = float.Parse(array[2], NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			lBasis.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			componentNames.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			codisp.Text = num.ToString("#0.000") + Environment.NewLine + num2.ToString("#0.000") + Environment.NewLine + num3.ToString("#0.000");
			LastPX = num;
			LastPY = num2;
			LastPZ = 1f - num2 - num;
			LastMX = e.ix;
			LastMY = e.iy;
		}

		private void _pfsplot1_OnClick(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_OnClickEvent e)
		{
			if (Highlight && LastID.Id != -1 && short.TryParse(LastID.UserData.ToString(), out var result))
			{
				_pfsplot1.SetObject(result, 3, 4);
				Highlight = false;
			}
			short ix = 0;
			if (_pfsplot1.GetPointerLocation(ref ix, ref ix))
			{
				switch (UserMode)
				{
				case DoingWhat.DW_SelectingMarkerPoint:
					MarkerAdd_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_SelectingCurvePoint:
					CurveAdd_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLine:
					DrawLine_Do(e.ix, e.iy);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLineEnd:
					DrawLine_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_SelectingTieLinePoint:
					TieLineAdd_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				}
			}
			ObjID objectSE = GetObjectSE(e.objectID, TPObjectsAdded);
			LastID = GetObjectSE(e.objectID, TPObjectsAdded);
			if (LastID.Id == -1)
			{
				LastID = GetObjectSE(e.objectID, TPUserAdded);
			}
			if (objectSE.What != (ObjType)(-1) && WhatsShowing[(int)objectSE.What])
			{
				switch (objectSE.What)
				{
				case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
					if (WhatsShowing[2])
					{
						_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)(int)objectSE.UserData, 5, 5);
						LastID = objectSE;
						Highlight = true;
					}
					else
					{
						Highlight = false;
					}
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkVLLE:
					if (WhatsShowing[7])
					{
						_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)(int)objectSE.UserData, 5, 5);
						LastID = objectSE;
						Highlight = true;
					}
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineUserLLE:
					_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)(int)objectSE.UserData, 5, 5);
					LastID = objectSE;
					Highlight = true;
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
					_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)(int)objectSE.UserData, 5, 5);
					LastID = objectSE;
					Highlight = true;
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkVLLEUser:
					if (WhatsShowing[8])
					{
						_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)(int)objectSE.UserData, 5, 5);
						LastID = objectSE;
						Highlight = true;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (objectSE.Id > 0)
			{
				LastID = objectSE;
			}
			if (!_pfsplot1.IsRegionDefined())
			{
				((base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu).Items[3] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			}
			if (e.objectID == 0)
			{
				LastID.Id = -1;
			}
			Refresh();
		}

		private void _pfsplot1_RegionDefined(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ContextMenu obj = base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu;
			MenuItem menuItem = obj.Items[3] as MenuItem;
			MenuItem menuItem2 = obj.Items[4] as MenuItem;
			MenuItem menuItem3 = obj.Items[5] as MenuItem;
			menuItem.IsEnabled = true;
			if (ZoomMode == 0.0)
			{
				menuItem2.IsEnabled = false;
				menuItem3.IsEnabled = false;
			}
			this.RegionDefined(this, new EventArgs());
		}

		private void _pfsplot1_OnRBClick(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_OnRBClickEvent e)
		{
			plotHost.Focus();
			if (e.objectID != 0)
			{
				LastID = GetObjectSE(e.objectID, TPObjectsAdded);
				if (LastID.Id == -1)
				{
					LastID = GetObjectSE(e.objectID, TPUserAdded);
				}
				if (!WhatsShowing[(int)LastID.What])
				{
					return;
				}
				ContextMenu contextMenu = base.Resources["mnuMarker"] as ContextMenu;
				ContextMenu contextMenu2 = base.Resources["mnuTieline"] as ContextMenu;
				ContextMenu contextMenu3 = base.Resources["mnuCurve"] as ContextMenu;
				switch (LastID.What)
				{
				case ObjType.MarkUserPoint:
				{
					if (LastID.UserData != null && (int)LastID.UserData != 0)
					{
						for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
						{
							(contextMenu.Items[i] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
						}
					}
					plotHost.ContextMenu = contextMenu;
					for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
					{
						(contextMenu.Items[j] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = true;
					}
					plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
					break;
				}
				case ObjType.TieLineLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
				case ObjType.FreeHandLine:
				case ObjType.TieLineUserLLE:
					plotHost.ContextMenu = contextMenu2;
					plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveResidue:
				case ObjType.CurveDistill:
					plotHost.ContextMenu = contextMenu3;
					plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				LastID.Id = -1;
				ContextMenu contextMenu4 = base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu;
				plotHost.ContextMenu = contextMenu4;
				plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				_pfsplot1.UpdateAxis();
				Refresh();
			}
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			_pfsplot1.Clear();
			RefreshComponents();
			_pfsplot1.SetObject(3, 1, 3);
			_pfsplot1.SetObject(2, 1, 3);
			_pfsplot1.SetSymbol("TempUnit", TempUnits);
			TPObjectsAdded.Clear();
			TPArray.Clear();
			TPUserAdded.Clear();
		}

		public bool DeleteItem(ObjID thing, bool force = false)
		{
			if (!force && (thing.What == ObjType.MarkVLLE || thing.What == ObjType.TextAzeotrope))
			{
				return false;
			}
			try
			{
				if (thing.What == ObjType.MarkUserPoint)
				{
					if (LastID.UserData != null && (int)LastID.UserData != 0)
					{
						RemoveLabelFromMarker();
					}
					_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)LastID.Id, -1, 0);
					TPObjectsAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == LastID.Id);
					TPUserAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == LastID.Id);
				}
				else if (thing.Id != -1 && thing.Id != 0)
				{
					if (thing.What == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE || thing.What == ObjType.TieLineUserLLE)
					{
						DeletUserTieLines(thing);
					}
					if (thing.What != ObjType.CurveDistillBound && thing.What != ObjType.CurveDistill && thing.What != ObjType.TextUser && thing.UserData != null && (int)thing.UserData != 0)
					{
						_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)(int)thing.UserData, -1, 0);
						TPObjectsAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == (int)thing.UserData);
						TPUserAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == (int)thing.UserData);
					}
					_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)thing.Id, -1, 0);
					TPObjectsAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == thing.Id);
					TPUserAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == thing.Id);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void DeletUserTieLines(ObjID thing)
		{
			_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)thing.Id, -1, 1);
		}

		private void RemoveLabelFromMarker(ObjID obj = null)
		{
			ObjID objID = ((obj != null) ? obj : LastID);
			if (objID.What == ObjType.MarkUserPoint)
			{
				ObjID tempObj = GetObjectSE((int)objID.UserData, TPUserAdded);
				DeleteItem(tempObj);
				tempObj.Id = objID.Id;
				tempObj.Pos = objID.Pos;
				tempObj.UserData = 0;
				tempObj.What = objID.What;
				TPUserAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == tempObj.Id);
				TPUserAdded.Add(tempObj);
			}
		}

		public void DisplayItem(bool state, ObjType thingType)
		{
			foreach (ObjID item in TPObjectsAdded)
			{
				CheckObjects(item, thingType, state);
			}
			foreach (ObjID item2 in TPUserAdded)
			{
				CheckObjects(item2, thingType, state);
			}
			_pfsplot1.Redraw();
		}

		public ObjID ObjectType(ObjType theType)
		{
			foreach (ObjID item in TPObjectsAdded)
			{
				if (item.What == theType)
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			foreach (ObjID item2 in TPUserAdded)
			{
				if (item2.What == theType)
				{
					return item2;
				}
			}
			return new ObjID
			{
				Id = -1,
				What = ObjType.None
			};
		}

		public void RemoveFromArray(ObjType index)
		{
			TPArray.RemoveAll((ArrayObj p) => p.Type == index);
		}

		public ArrayObj FindArray(ObjType index)
		{
			return TPArray.Find((ArrayObj p) => p.Type == index);
		}

		public void PlotEvenlope(object obj, ObjType envelopeType)
		{
			if (obj is double[,,] array)
			{
				RemoveFromArray(envelopeType);
				ArrayObj item = new ArrayObj
				{
					Arr = array.Clone(),
					Type = envelopeType
				};
				TPArray.Add(item);
				for (int i = 0; i <= array.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
				{
					ObjID objID = new ObjID();
					objID.Id = _pfsplot1.NewPolygon((short)Properties.PEnvelope.PColour, (short)Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern);
					_pfsplot1.SetLineAttribute((short)objID.Id, (short)Properties.PEnvelope.PColour, (short)Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern, 0, 0, 2, 31);
					_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)objID.Id, 1, 3);
					for (int j = 0; j <= array.GetUpperBound(1) && (int)array[i, j, CO1] != -1; j++)
					{
						double[] array2 = new double[3];
						Transformers(array[i, j, CO1], array[i, j, CO2], ref array2[0], ref array2[1], ref array2[2]);
						_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID.Id, (short)j, array2[CO1], array2[CO2]);
					}
					objID.What = envelopeType;
					TPObjectsAdded.Add(objID);
				}
			}
			if (!WhatsShowing[(int)envelopeType])
			{
				DisplayItem(state: false, envelopeType);
			}
		}

		public void PlotTieLine(object obj, ObjType lineType, object objTieLineMark = null)
		{
			double[,] array = obj as double[,];
			double[,] array2 = objTieLineMark as double[,];
			if (array != null)
			{
				if (lineType == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE || lineType == ObjType.TieLineUserLLE)
				{
					ArrayObj arrayObj = FindArray(lineType);
					RemoveFromArray(lineType);
					if (arrayObj != null)
					{
						double[,] array3 = (arrayObj.Arr as double[,]).Clone() as double[,];
						double[,] array4 = new double[(arrayObj.Arr as double[,]).GetUpperBound(0) + 3, 3];
						for (int i = 0; i <= array3.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
						{
							for (int j = 0; j <= 2; j++)
							{
								array4[i, j] = array3[i, j];
							}
						}
						for (int k = 0; k <= 1; k++)
						{
							for (int l = 0; l <= 2; l++)
							{
								array4[array4.GetUpperBound(0) - 1 + k, l] = array[k, l];
							}
						}
						array = array4.Clone() as double[,];
					}
					if (array2 != null)
					{
						arrayObj = FindArray(ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
						RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
						if (arrayObj != null)
						{
							double[,] array3 = arrayObj.Arr as double[,];
							double[,] array4 = new double[(arrayObj.Arr as double[,]).GetUpperBound(0) + 2, 3];
							for (int m = 0; m <= array3.GetUpperBound(0); m++)
							{
								for (int n = 0; n <= 2; n++)
								{
									array4[m, n] = array3[m, n];
								}
							}
							for (int num = 0; num <= 1; num++)
							{
								array4[array4.GetUpperBound(0), num] = array2[0, num];
							}
							array2 = array4.Clone() as double[,];
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					RemoveFromArray(lineType);
					RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLE);
				}
				TPArray.Add(new ArrayObj
				{
					Arr = array.Clone(),
					Type = lineType
				});
				if (array2 != null)
				{
					ArrayObj arrayObj2 = new ArrayObj
					{
						Arr = array2.Clone()
					};
					if (lineType == ObjType.TieLineVLLE)
					{
						arrayObj2.Type = ObjType.MarkVLLE;
					}
					else
					{
						arrayObj2.Type = ObjType.MarkVLLEUser;
					}
					arrayObj2.Arr = array2.Clone();
					TPArray.Add(arrayObj2);
				}
				short color = (short)Properties.PTieLine.PColour;
				int num2 = 0;
				for (int num3 = 0; num3 <= array.GetUpperBound(0); num3 += 2)
				{
					ObjID objID = new ObjID();
					objID.Id = _pfsplot1.NewLine(color, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PLine, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize, 0);
					if (WhatsShowing[4])
					{
						_pfsplot1.SetLineAttribute((short)objID.Id, color, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PLine, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize, 0, 31);
					}
					else
					{
						_pfsplot1.SetLineAttribute((short)objID.Id, color, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PLine, 0, 0, 0, 31);
					}
					double[] array5 = new double[3];
					Transformers(array[num3, CO1], array[num3, CO2], ref array5[0], ref array5[1], ref array5[2]);
					_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID.Id, 0, array5[CO1], array5[CO2]);
					Transformers(array[num3 + 1, CO1], array[num3 + 1, CO2], ref array5[0], ref array5[1], ref array5[2]);
					_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID.Id, 1, array5[CO1], array5[CO2]);
					double[,] array6 = new double[2, 3];
					double[] array7 = new double[3];
					for (int num4 = 0; num4 < 2; num4++)
					{
						for (int num5 = 0; num5 < 3; num5++)
						{
							array6[num4, num5] = array[num3 + num4, num5];
						}
					}
					if (array2 != null)
					{
						ObjID objID2 = new ObjID();
						objID2.Id = _pfsplot1.NewLine(color, -1, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize, 0);
						Transformers(array2[num2, CO1], array2[num2, CO2], ref array5[0], ref array5[1], ref array5[2]);
						_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID2.Id, 0, array5[CO1], array5[CO2]);
						for (int num6 = 0; num6 < 3; num6++)
						{
							array7[num6] = array2[num2, num6];
						}
						num2++;
						switch (lineType)
						{
						case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
							objID2.What = ObjType.MarkVLLE;
							break;
						case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
							objID2.What = ObjType.MarkVLLEUser;
							break;
						}
						objID.UserData = objID2.Id;
						objID2.UserData = objID.Id;
						objID2.Pos = array7.Clone();
						TPObjectsAdded.Add(objID2);
					}
					objID.What = lineType;
					objID.Pos = array6.Clone();
					TPObjectsAdded.Add(objID);
				}
			}
			if (!WhatsShowing[(int)lineType])
			{
				DisplayItem(state: false, lineType);
				if (lineType == ObjType.TieLineVLLE || lineType == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE)
				{
					WhatsShowing[2] = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DisplayItem(state: true, lineType);
			}
			bool state = WhatsShowing[2];
			DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkVLLE);
			DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
		}

		public void PlotAzeotropes(double[,] azeoArray, int azeoNum, double[] tempArray)
		{
			if (azeoArray != null && tempArray != null)
			{
				ArrayObj arrayObj = new ArrayObj();
				arrayObj.Arr = azeoArray.Clone();
				arrayObj.Type = ObjType.MarkAzeotrope;
				TPArray.Add(arrayObj);
				arrayObj = new ArrayObj();
				arrayObj.Arr = tempArray.Clone();
				arrayObj.Type = ObjType.TextAzeotrope;
				TPArray.Add(arrayObj);
				double[] array = new double[3];
				for (int i = 0; i < azeoNum; i++)
				{
					Transformers(azeoArray[i, CO1], azeoArray[i, CO2], ref array[0], ref array[1], ref array[2]);
					ObjID objID = MarkerAdd(Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour, Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker, Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize, array[CO1], array[CO2]);
					_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)objID.Id, 1, 3);
					objID.What = ObjType.MarkAzeotrope;
					TPObjectsAdded.Add(objID.Clone() as ObjID);
					objID.Id = _pfsplot1.newText(AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(tempArray[i], TempUnits, showUnit: true), array[CO1], array[CO2], 3);
					_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)objID.Id, 8, 8);
					_pfsplot1.SetTextAttribute((short)objID.Id, 0, 1, 0, Properties.PFont.PName, (short)Properties.PFont.PSize, 0);
					objID.What = ObjType.TextAzeotrope;
					TPObjectsAdded.Add(objID.Clone() as ObjID);
				}
			}
			if (!WhatsShowing[1])
			{
				DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.MarkAzeotrope);
			}
		}

		public ObjID MarkerAdd(TPColour color, TPMarkerShape mkr, int sZ, double X, double Y)
		{
			ObjID objID = new ObjID();
			objID.Id = _pfsplot1.NewLine((short)color, -1, (short)mkr, (short)sZ, 0);
			_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID.Id, 0, X, Y);
			return objID;
		}

		public void MarkerText(string text)
		{
			ObjID objID = new ObjID();
			objID.Id = _pfsplot1.newText(text, LastPX, LastPY, 3);
			_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)objID.Id, 8, 8);
			_pfsplot1.SetTextAttribute((short)objID.Id, 0, 1, 0, Properties.PFont.PName, (short)Properties.PFont.PSize, 0);
			objID.What = ObjType.TextUser;
			objID.UserData = text;
			TPUserAdded.Add(objID);
			LastID.UserData = objID.Id;
			TPUserAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == LastID.Id);
			TPUserAdded.Add(LastID);
			Refresh();
		}

		public void PlotDistillationBoundary(double[,,] points, int[] dir)
		{
			double[,] array = new double[points.GetUpperBound(1) + 1, 3];
			for (int i = 0; i <= points.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j <= points.GetUpperBound(1); j++)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k <= 2; k++)
					{
						array[j, k] = points[i, j, k];
					}
				}
				PlotCurve(array, ObjType.CurveDistillBound, dir[i]);
			}
			if (!WhatsShowing[15])
			{
				DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
			}
		}

		public void PlotCurve(double[,] curveArray, ObjType curveType, int dir)
		{
			TPColour tPColour = TPColour.Black;
			TPLine tPLine = TPLine.Dash;
			TPMarkerShape tPMarkerShape = TPMarkerShape.MK_Cross;
			int num = 0;
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3];
			if (curveArray != null)
			{
				switch (curveType)
				{
				case ObjType.CurveResidue:
					tPColour = Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour;
					tPLine = Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine;
					tPMarkerShape = Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker;
					num = Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize;
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveDistill:
					tPColour = Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour;
					tPLine = Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine;
					tPMarkerShape = Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker;
					num = Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize;
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveDistillBound:
					tPColour = Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour;
					tPLine = Properties.PDistBoundry.PLine;
					tPMarkerShape = Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker;
					num = Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize;
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveIso:
					tPColour = Properties.PIsoCurve.PColour;
					tPLine = Properties.PIsoCurve.PLine;
					tPMarkerShape = Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarker;
					num = Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarkerSize;
					break;
				}
				ObjID objID = new ObjID();
				objID.Id = _pfsplot1.NewLine((short)tPColour, (short)tPLine, (short)tPMarkerShape, (short)num, (short)dir);
				for (int i = 0; i <= curveArray.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
				{
					if (curveArray[i, CO1] >= -1E-06 && curveArray[i, CO2] <= 1.00001)
					{
						Composition_Array[0] = curveArray[i, 0];
						Composition_Array[1] = curveArray[i, 1];
						Composition_Array[2] = curveArray[i, 2];
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, (BasisType)BasisType, 3);
						_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID.Id, (short)i, Composition_Array[CO1], Composition_Array[CO2]);
						if (curveType == ObjType.CurveDistillBound || curveType == ObjType.CurveIso)
						{
							_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)objID.Id, 1, 3);
						}
					}
				}
				if (curveType == ObjType.CurveDistill || curveType == ObjType.CurveResidue)
				{
					objID.What = curveType;
					objID.Pos = curveArray.Clone();
					objID.UserData = dir;
					TPUserAdded.Add(objID);
				}
				else
				{
					objID.What = curveType;
					objID.Pos = curveArray.Clone();
					objID.UserData = dir;
					TPObjectsAdded.Add(objID);
				}
			}
			if (!WhatsShowing[(int)curveType])
			{
				DisplayItem(state: false, curveType);
			}
		}

		public void PlotIsoCurve(double[,,] points)
		{
			double[,] array = new double[points.GetUpperBound(1) + 1, 3];
			for (int i = 0; i <= points.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j <= points.GetUpperBound(1); j++)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k <= 2; k++)
					{
						array[j, k] = points[i, j, k];
					}
				}
				PlotCurve(array, ObjType.CurveIso, 0);
			}
			if (!WhatsShowing[19])
			{
				DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveIso);
			}
		}

		public void FinishedAdding()
		{
			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			MousePointer(1);
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(2, onOff: true, @default: true);
			AddManyTimes = false;
		}

		public void ShowBalloon(string message)
		{
			_pfsplot1.DisplayText(message, (short)LastMX, (short)(LastMY + 20.0), 30000);
		}

		public void SetViewStatus(ObjType item, bool stat)
		{
			WhatsShowing[(int)item] = stat;
		}

		public void SetCoordinates()
		{
			switch (CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.XY:
				CO1 = 0;
				CO2 = 1;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				CO1 = 0;
				CO2 = 2;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				CO1 = 1;
				CO2 = 0;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				CO1 = 1;
				CO2 = 2;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				CO1 = 2;
				CO2 = 0;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				CO1 = 2;
				CO2 = 1;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void RotatePoints()
		{
			switch (CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.XY:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.YZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.ZX;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.XY;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.XZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.ZY;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.YX;
				break;
			}
			string[] array = new string[2]
			{
				Labels[0, 0],
				Labels[0, 1]
			};
			Labels[0, 0] = Labels[1, 0];
			Labels[0, 1] = Labels[1, 1];
			Labels[1, 0] = Labels[2, 0];
			Labels[1, 1] = Labels[2, 1];
			Labels[2, 0] = array[0];
			Labels[2, 1] = array[1];
			double num = TPPureTemp[0];
			TPPureTemp[0] = TPPureTemp[1];
			TPPureTemp[1] = TPPureTemp[2];
			TPPureTemp[2] = num;
			SetCoordinates();
			RefreshComponents();
			Redraw();
		}

		public void FlipPoints()
		{
			switch (CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.XY:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.ZY;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.YZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.ZX;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.XZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.YX;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				CoordMode = CoordPair.XY;
				break;
			}
			string[] array = new string[2]
			{
				Labels[0, 0],
				Labels[0, 1]
			};
			Labels[0, 0] = Labels[2, 0];
			Labels[0, 1] = Labels[2, 1];
			Labels[2, 0] = array[0];
			Labels[2, 1] = array[1];
			double num = TPPureTemp[0];
			TPPureTemp[0] = TPPureTemp[2];
			TPPureTemp[2] = num;
			SetCoordinates();
			RefreshComponents();
			Redraw();
		}

		public void Redraw(bool bConvertBasis = false)
		{
			List<ArrayObj> list = new List<ArrayObj>(TPArray);
			List<ObjID> list2 = new List<ObjID>(TPObjectsAdded);
			List<ObjID> list3 = new List<ObjID>(TPUserAdded);
			Highlight = false;
			LastID.Id = -1;
			bool flag = false;
			Clear();
			PlotZoomFull();
			object obj = null;
			SetPureTemperatures();
			foreach (ArrayObj item in list)
			{
				switch (item.Type)
				{
				case ObjType.MarkAzeotrope:
					obj = item.Arr as double[,];
					break;
				case ObjType.TextAzeotrope:
					PlotAzeotropes((double[,])obj, (obj as double[,]).GetUpperBound(0) + 1, item.Arr as double[]);
					obj = null;
					break;
				case ObjType.EnvLLE:
					PlotEvenlope(item.Arr, ObjType.EnvLLE);
					break;
				case ObjType.EnvVLLE:
					PlotEvenlope(item.Arr, ObjType.EnvVLLE);
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineLLE:
					PlotTieLine(item.Arr, ObjType.TieLineLLE);
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineUserLLE:
					PlotTieLine(item.Arr, ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
					obj = item.Arr;
					flag = true;
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkVLLEUser:
					if (flag)
					{
						PlotTieLine(obj, ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE, item.Arr);
						obj = null;
						flag = false;
					}
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
					obj = item.Arr;
					flag = true;
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkVLLE:
					if (flag)
					{
						PlotTieLine(obj, ObjType.TieLineVLLE, item.Arr);
						obj = null;
						flag = false;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			foreach (ObjID item2 in list2)
			{
				switch (item2.What)
				{
				case ObjType.CurveDistillBound:
					PlotCurve((double[,])item2.Pos, ObjType.CurveDistillBound, (int)item2.UserData);
					if (!WhatsShowing[15])
					{
						DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
					}
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveIso:
					PlotCurve((double[,])item2.Pos, ObjType.CurveIso, 0);
					if (!WhatsShowing[19])
					{
						DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveIso);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3];
			double[] Composition_Array2 = new double[3];
			foreach (ObjID item3 in list3)
			{
				switch (item3.What)
				{
				case ObjType.MarkUserPoint:
				{
					double[] Composition_Array3 = item3.Pos as double[];
					if (bConvertBasis)
					{
						if (BasisType == 1)
						{
							AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array3, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
						}
						else
						{
							AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array3, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mole, 3);
						}
					}
					obj = Composition_Array3;
					ObjID objID2 = MarkerAdd(Properties.PUserMarker.PColour, Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker, (short)Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize, Composition_Array3[CO1], Composition_Array3[CO2]);
					objID2.What = ObjType.MarkUserPoint;
					objID2.Pos = item3.Pos;
					objID2.UserData = item3.UserData;
					LastID = objID2.Clone() as ObjID;
					TPUserAdded.Add(objID2.Clone() as ObjID);
					if (LastID.UserData == null || (int)LastID.UserData == 0)
					{
						break;
					}
					LastPX = (float)Composition_Array3[CO1];
					LastPY = (float)Composition_Array3[CO2];
					objID2 = GetObjectSE((int)LastID.UserData, list3);
					if (objID2 == null || objID2.Id == -1 || objID2.UserData == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (objID2.What == ObjType.TextUser)
					{
						string text = objID2.UserData.ToString();
						string unit = text.Substring(text.Length - 1);
						if (double.TryParse(text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1), out var result))
						{
							double dbTemp = AzeoGUIGlobals.KTemp(result, unit);
							objID2.UserData = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(dbTemp, _tempUnit, showUnit: true);
						}
					}
					MarkerText(objID2.UserData.ToString());
					break;
				}
				case ObjType.CurveResidue:
				{
					double[,] curveArray = item3.Pos as double[,];
					PlotCurve(curveArray, ObjType.CurveResidue, (int)item3.UserData);
					if (!WhatsShowing[13])
					{
						DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveResidue);
					}
					break;
				}
				case ObjType.CurveDistill:
				{
					double[,] curveArray2 = item3.Pos as double[,];
					PlotCurve(curveArray2, ObjType.CurveDistill, (int)item3.UserData);
					if (!WhatsShowing[14])
					{
						DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveDistill);
					}
					break;
				}
				case ObjType.CurveDistillBound:
					PlotCurve((double[,])item3.Pos, ObjType.CurveDistillBound, (int)item3.UserData);
					if (!WhatsShowing[15])
					{
						DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
					}
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveIso:
					PlotCurve((double[,])item3.Pos, ObjType.CurveIso, 0);
					if (!WhatsShowing[19])
					{
						DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.CurveIso);
					}
					break;
				case ObjType.FreeHandLine:
				{
					double[,] array = item3.Pos as double[,];
					if (bConvertBasis)
					{
						Composition_Array[0] = array[0, 0];
						Composition_Array[1] = array[0, 1];
						Composition_Array[2] = array[0, 2];
						Composition_Array2[0] = array[1, 0];
						Composition_Array2[1] = array[1, 1];
						Composition_Array2[2] = array[1, 2];
						if (BasisType == 1)
						{
							AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
							AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array2, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
						}
						else
						{
							AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mole, 3);
							AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array2, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mole, 3);
						}
						array[0, 0] = Composition_Array[0];
						array[0, 1] = Composition_Array[1];
						array[0, 2] = Composition_Array[2];
						array[1, 0] = Composition_Array2[0];
						array[1, 1] = Composition_Array2[1];
						array[1, 2] = Composition_Array2[2];
					}
					ObjID objID = PlotFreeLine(array);
					objID.What = ObjType.FreeHandLine;
					objID.Pos = item3.Pos;
					TPUserAdded.Add(objID);
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			ContextMenu obj2 = base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu;
			MenuItem menuItem = obj2.Items[7] as MenuItem;
			if (BasisType == 1)
			{
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ChangeToMolarBasis;
				lBasis.Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassBasis;
			}
			else
			{
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ChangeToMassBasis;
				lBasis.Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleBasis;
			}
			MenuItem menuItem2 = obj2.Items[8] as MenuItem;
			for (int i = 0; i <= 3; i++)
			{
				MenuItem menuItem3 = menuItem2.Items[i] as MenuItem;
				if (TempUnits.Equals(menuItem3.Header))
				{
					menuItem3.IsEnabled = false;
				}
				else
				{
					menuItem3.IsEnabled = true;
				}
			}
			Refresh();
		}

		private ObjID PlotFreeLine(double[,] points)
		{
			double[] array = new double[3];
			ObjID objID = new ObjID();
			objID.Id = _pfsplot1.NewLine((short)Properties.PUserLine.PColour, (short)Properties.PUserLine.PLine, (short)Properties.PUserLine.PMarker, (short)Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize, 0);
			array[0] = points[0, 0];
			array[1] = points[0, 1];
			array[2] = points[0, 2];
			_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID.Id, 0, array[CO1], array[CO2]);
			array[0] = points[1, 0];
			array[1] = points[1, 1];
			array[2] = points[1, 2];
			_pfsplot1.SetXYData((short)objID.Id, 1, array[CO1], array[CO2]);
			return objID;
		}

		public void SetPureTemperatures(double? comp1 = null, double? comp2 = null, double? comp3 = null, int? use_basis = null)
		{
			if (comp1.HasValue && comp2.HasValue && comp3.HasValue)
			{
				TPPureTemp[0] = comp1.Value;
				TPPureTemp[1] = comp2.Value;
				TPPureTemp[2] = comp3.Value;
			}
			double[] array = TPPureTemp.Clone() as double[];
			if (use_basis.HasValue)
			{
				switch (use_basis)
				{
				case 1:
					array[0] = TPPureTemp[0];
					array[1] = TPPureTemp[1];
					array[2] = TPPureTemp[2];
					break;
				case 2:
					array[0] = TPPureTemp[0];
					array[1] = TPPureTemp[2];
					array[2] = TPPureTemp[1];
					break;
				case 3:
					array[0] = TPPureTemp[1];
					array[1] = TPPureTemp[0];
					array[2] = TPPureTemp[2];
					break;
				case 4:
					array[0] = TPPureTemp[1];
					array[1] = TPPureTemp[2];
					array[2] = TPPureTemp[0];
					break;
				case 5:
					array[0] = TPPureTemp[2];
					array[1] = TPPureTemp[0];
					array[2] = TPPureTemp[1];
					break;
				case 6:
					array[0] = TPPureTemp[2];
					array[1] = TPPureTemp[1];
					array[2] = TPPureTemp[0];
					break;
				}
			}
			Labels[0, 1] = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(array[0], TempUnits);
			Labels[1, 1] = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(array[1], TempUnits);
			Labels[2, 1] = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(array[2], TempUnits);
			_pfsplot1.SetSymbol("PTempX", Labels[0, 1]);
			_pfsplot1.SetSymbol("PTempY", Labels[1, 1]);
			_pfsplot1.SetSymbol("PTempZ", Labels[2, 1]);
			TPPureTemp[0] = array[0];
			TPPureTemp[1] = array[1];
			TPPureTemp[2] = array[2];
		}

		public void ClearPlot()
		{
			_pfsplot1.Clear();
			RefreshComponents();
			_pfsplot1.SetObject(3, 1, 3);
			_pfsplot1.SetObject(2, 1, 3);
			_pfsplot1.SetSymbol("TempUnit", TempUnits);
		}

		public void Refresh()
		{
			_pfsplot1.UpdateAxis();
		}

		public void MousePointer(short shape)
		{
			_pfsplot1.SetMousePointer(shape);
		}

		public void RemoveAllLabelsFromMarker()
		{
			List<ObjID> list = new List<ObjID>();
			foreach (ObjID item in TPUserAdded.FindAll((ObjID p) => p.What == ObjType.MarkUserPoint))
			{
				if (int.TryParse(item.UserData.ToString(), out var result))
				{
					ObjID tempObj = GetObjectSE(result, TPUserAdded);
					DeleteItem(tempObj);
					tempObj.Id = item.Id;
					tempObj.Pos = item.Pos;
					tempObj.UserData = 0;
					tempObj.What = item.What;
					TPUserAdded.RemoveAll((ObjID p) => p.Id == tempObj.Id);
					list.Add(tempObj);
				}
			}
			TPUserAdded.AddRange(list);
			Refresh();
		}

		private static int GetIntValueFromObj(object obj)
		{
			int.TryParse(obj.ToString(), out var result);
			return result;
		}

		private void RefreshComponents()
		{
			componentNames.Text = Labels[0, 0] + Environment.NewLine + Labels[1, 0] + Environment.NewLine + Labels[2, 0];
			RemoveRightAngleLabels();
			if (PlotType == PlotType.Equilateral)
			{
				RemoveRightAngleLabels();
			}
			else
			{
				AddRightAngleLabels();
			}
		}

		private void AddRightAngleLabels()
		{
			string text = Labels[0, 0];
			if (WhatsShowing[17])
			{
				text += "\\n(&[PTempX] &[TempUnit])";
			}
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(-2, text);
			text = Labels[2, 0];
			if (WhatsShowing[17])
			{
				text += "\\n(&[PTempZ] &[TempUnit])";
			}
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(-1, text);
			text = Labels[1, 0];
			if (WhatsShowing[17])
			{
				text += "(&[PTempY] &[TempUnit])";
			}
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(-3, text);
			_pfsplot1.xlabel = "";
			_pfsplot1.ylabel = "";
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(2, "");
		}

		private void RemoveRightAngleLabels()
		{
			_pfsplot1.xlabel = Labels[0, 0];
			_pfsplot1.ylabel = Labels[1, 0];
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(2, Labels[2, 0]);
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(-1, " ");
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(-2, " ");
			_pfsplot1.SetLabel(-3, " ");
		}

			CoDisplay = 1;
			CalcBoilPoint = 0;
			TransparentLabels = 0;
			ToolBarPos = 4;
		}

		private ObjID GetObjectSE(int id, IEnumerable<ObjID> thisOne)
		{
			foreach (ObjID item in thisOne)
			{
				if (item != null && item.Id == id)
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return new ObjID
			{
				Id = -1,
				What = ObjType.None
			};
		}

		private void CheckObjects(ObjID iObjId, ObjType thingType, bool state)
		{
			short value = (state ? ((short)1) : ((short)0));
			if (iObjId.What == thingType)
			{
				_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)iObjId.Id, value, 1);
				SetViewStatus(thingType, state);
			}
			if (thingType == ObjType.TextAzeotrope && PlotType == PlotType.RightAngled)
			{
				RemoveRightAngleLabels();
				AddRightAngleLabels();
			}
			if (thingType == ObjType.MarkAzeotrope && iObjId.What == ObjType.TextAzeotrope)
			{
				_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)iObjId.Id, value, 1);
			}
			if (thingType == ObjType.TieLineVLLE && iObjId.What == ObjType.MarkVLLE)
			{
				_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)iObjId.Id, value, 1);
				SetViewStatus(thingType, state);
			}
			if (thingType == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE && iObjId.What == ObjType.MarkVLLEUser)
			{
				_pfsplot1.SetObject((short)iObjId.Id, value, 1);
				SetViewStatus(thingType, state);
			}
			if (thingType == ObjType.MarkTieLine && (iObjId.What == ObjType.TieLineVLLE || iObjId.What == ObjType.TieLineLLE || iObjId.What == ObjType.TieLineUserLLE || iObjId.What == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE))
			{
				if (!state)
				{
					_pfsplot1.SetLineAttribute((short)iObjId.Id, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 12);
				}
				else
				{
					_pfsplot1.SetLineAttribute((short)iObjId.Id, 0, 0, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize, 0, 12);
				}
				SetViewStatus(ObjType.MarkTieLine, state);
			}
			if (thingType == ObjType.MarkDistillCurve && iObjId.What == ObjType.CurveDistill)
			{
				if (!state)
				{
					_pfsplot1.SetLineAttribute((short)iObjId.Id, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 12);
				}
				else
				{
					_pfsplot1.SetLineAttribute((short)iObjId.Id, 0, 0, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker, (short)Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize, 0, 12);
				}
				SetViewStatus(ObjType.MarkDistillCurve, state);
			}
		}

		private void Transformers(dynamic c1, dynamic c2, ref double X, ref double Y, ref double Z)
		{
			ProcessCoordinates(c1, c2, ref X, ref Y, ref Z);
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3] { X, Y, Z };
			AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, (BasisType)BasisType, 3);
			X = Composition_Array[0];
			Y = Composition_Array[1];
			Z = Composition_Array[2];
		}

		public void ProcessCoordinates(dynamic c1, dynamic c2, ref double X, ref double Y, ref double Z)
		{
			double num = 1 - c1 - c2;
			switch (CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.XY:
				X = c1;
				Y = c2;
				Z = num;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				X = c1;
				Y = num;
				Z = c2;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				X = c2;
				Y = c1;
				Z = num;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				X = num;
				Y = c1;
				Z = c2;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				X = c2;
				Y = num;
				Z = c1;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				X = num;
				Y = c2;
				Z = c1;
				break;
			}
		}

		private void SortCoords(ref double X, ref double Y, ref double Z)
		{
			switch (CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.XZ:
			{
				double num = Y;
				Y = Z;
				Z = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.YX:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Y;
				Y = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.YZ:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Z;
				Z = Y;
				Y = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.ZX:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Y;
				Y = Z;
				Z = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.ZY:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Z;
				Z = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.XY:
				break;
			}
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			_pfsplot1.Dispose();
		}

		public void TieLineAdd_Start(bool bAddMany)
		{
			MousePointer(2);
			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_SelectingTieLinePoint;
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(2, onOff: true, @default: false);
			AddManyTimes = bAddMany;
		}

		public void TieLineAdd_End(double X, double Y)
		{
			if (!AddManyTimes)
			{
				FinishedAdding();
			}
			double num = 1.0 - X - Y;
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3] { X, Y, num };
			SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
			if (BasisType != 2)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mole, 3);
			}
			this.AddTieLine(this, new AddTieLineArgs(Composition_Array[0], Composition_Array[1], Composition_Array[2]));
		}

		public void TielineAddValue_Start()
		{
			AddByValue addByValue = new AddByValue((BasisType)BasisType, Labels[0, 0], Labels[1, 0], Labels[2, 0], Window.GetWindow(this));
			bool? flag = addByValue.ShowDialog();
			if (!flag.HasValue || !flag.Value)
			{
				return;
			}
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3]
			{
				addByValue.CompVal0.HasValue ? addByValue.CompVal0.Value : 0.0,
				addByValue.CompVal1.HasValue ? addByValue.CompVal1.Value : 0.0,
				addByValue.CompVal2.HasValue ? addByValue.CompVal2.Value : 0.0
			};
			SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
			if (addByValue.ABV_Basis != (BasisType)BasisType)
			{
				if (BasisType == 1)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
				}
				else
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mole, 3);
				}
			}
			TieLineAdd_End(Composition_Array[CO1], Composition_Array[CO2]);
		}

		public void CurveAdd_Start(bool bAddMany)

			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_SelectingCurvePoint;

		}

		public void MarkerAdd_Start()
		{
			MousePointer(2);
			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_SelectingMarkerPoint;
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(2, onOff: true, @default: false);
		}

		public void MarkerAdd_End(double X, double Y)
		{
			double[] array = new double[3]
			{
				X,
				Y,
				1.0 - X - Y
			};
			ObjID objID = MarkerAdd(Properties.PUserMarker.PColour, Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker, Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize, X, Y);
			objID.What = ObjType.MarkUserPoint;
			SortCoords(ref array[0], ref array[1], ref array[2]);
			objID.UserData = 0;
			objID.Pos = array;
			TPUserAdded.Add(objID);
			Refresh();
			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			MousePointer(1);
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(2, onOff: true, @default: true);
			this.UnpressButton(this, new UnpressButtonEventArgs("Marker"));
		}

		public bool MarkerAddText()
		{
			AddMarkerText addMarkerText = new AddMarkerText();
			if (addMarkerText.ShowDialog().Value)
			{
				MarkerText(addMarkerText.MarkerText);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void DrawLine_Start()
		{
			MousePointer(2);
			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLine;
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(2, onOff: true, @default: false);
		}

		public void DrawLine_Do(double X, double Y)
		{
			LineStart.X = (short)X;
			LineStart.Y = (short)Y;
			_pfsplot1.DragLine(LineStart.X, LineStart.Y, (short)X, (short)Y, 3);
			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLineEnd;
		}

		public void DrawLine_End(double X, double Y)
		{
			double[,] array = new double[2, 3];
			_pfsplot1.ConvertToPlot(LineStart.X, LineStart.Y, ref array[0, 0], ref array[0, 1]);
			array[0, 2] = 1.0 - array[0, 0] - array[0, 1];
			array[1, 0] = X;
			array[1, 1] = Y;
			array[1, 2] = 1.0 - X - Y;
			SortCoords(ref array[0, 0], ref array[0, 1], ref array[0, 2]);
			SortCoords(ref array[1, 0], ref array[1, 1], ref array[1, 2]);
			ObjID objID = PlotFreeLine(array);
			objID.What = ObjType.FreeHandLine;
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3]
			{
				array[0, 0],
				array[0, 1],
				array[0, 2]
			};
			double[] Composition_Array2 = new double[3]
			{
				array[1, 0],
				array[1, 1],
				array[1, 2]
			};
			if (BasisType != 2)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mole, 3);
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array2, ref Mol_Wt, AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mole, 3);
			}
			array[0, 0] = Composition_Array[0];
			array[0, 1] = Composition_Array[1];
			array[0, 2] = Composition_Array[2];
			array[1, 0] = Composition_Array2[0];
			array[1, 1] = Composition_Array2[1];
			array[1, 2] = Composition_Array2[2];
			objID.Pos = array;
			TPUserAdded.Add(objID);
			Refresh();
			UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			MousePointer(1);
			_pfsplot1.SetEvent(2, onOff: true, @default: true);
			this.UnpressButton(this, new UnpressButtonEventArgs("DrawLine"));
		}

		public void PrintPlot()
		{
			//IL_002a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				_pfsplot1.Print2(setupOnly: false);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.PrintError, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ProductTitle, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void Copy()
		{
			string text = ((TPBasis != AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.DataHelper.BasisType.BT_Mass) ? "Mole" : "Mass");
			_pfsplot1.SetFont(0, "", 0, 0);
			_pfsplot1.Title = "Ternary Map (" + text + " Basis)";
			_pfsplot1.Copy();
			_pfsplot1.SetFont(0, "", 1, 0);
			_pfsplot1.Title = "";
		}

		public void SwapPlotType()
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu).Items[9] as MenuItem;
			switch (PlotType)
			{
			case PlotType.Equilateral:
				AddRightAngleLabels();
				PlotType = PlotType.RightAngled;
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SwitchToEnquilateral;
				break;
			case PlotType.RightAngled:
				RemoveRightAngleLabels();
				PlotType = PlotType.Equilateral;
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SwitchToRightTriangle;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void ZoomInRegion()
		{
			if (_pfsplot1.IsRegionDefined())
			{
				double lowValue = 0.0;
				_pfsplot1.Zoom(4);
				_pfsplot1.GetAxisRange(0, ref lowValue, ref ZoomMode);
				ZoomMode -= lowValue;
				this.ZoomIn(this, new EventArgs());
			}
			ContextMenu obj = base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu;
			(obj.Items[3] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			(obj.Items[4] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = true;
			(obj.Items[5] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = true;
		}

		public void ZoomOutRegion()
		{
			double lowValue = 0.0;
			_pfsplot1.Zoom(2);
			_pfsplot1.GetAxisRange(0, ref lowValue, ref ZoomMode);
			ZoomMode -= lowValue;
			if (ZoomMode == 1.0)
			{
				ContextMenu obj = base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu;
				(obj.Items[4] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
				(obj.Items[5] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
				this.ZoomFull(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public void PlotZoomFull()
		{
			if (ZoomMode != 1.0)
			{
				_pfsplot1.Zoom(3);
				ZoomMode = 1.0;
				ContextMenu obj = base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu;
				(obj.Items[4] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
				(obj.Items[5] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
				this.ZoomFull(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void mnuStdChangeBasis_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu).Items[7] as MenuItem;
			if (BasisType == 2)
			{
				BasisType = 1;
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ChangeToMolarBasis;
				lBasis.Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassBasis;
			}
			else
			{
				BasisType = 2;
				menuItem.Header = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ChangeToMassBasis;
				lBasis.Text = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleBasis;
			}
			Redraw(bConvertBasis: true);
		}

		private void mnuStdTempUnitsR_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)

			MenuItem menuItem = (base.Resources["mnuStandard"] as ContextMenu).Items[8] as MenuItem;
			for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
			{
				(menuItem.Items[i] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = true;
			}
			TempUnits = "R";
			(menuItem.Items[0] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
			Redraw();
		}

		private void ShowPropertiesWindow(PropertyPages startPage)
		{
			bool coDisplay = CoDisplay != 0;
			bool calcBoilPoint = CalcBoilPoint != 0;
			bool transparentLabels = TransparentLabels != 0;
			PropertiesDlg propertiesDlg = new PropertiesDlg(startPage, Properties, coDisplay, calcBoilPoint, transparentLabels, ToolBarPos)
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow(this)
			};
			propertiesDlg.ShowDialog();
			if (propertiesDlg.AjustSettings == AjustSettings.AdjustNo)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (propertiesDlg.AjustSettings == AjustSettings.AdjustDefault)
			{
				DefaultProperties();
			}
			else
			{
				CoDisplay = (propertiesDlg.IsGlobal1Checked ? 1 : 0);
				CalcBoilPoint = (propertiesDlg.IsGlobal2Checked ? 1 : 0);
				TransparentLabels = (propertiesDlg.IsGlobal3Checked ? 1 : 0);
				ToolBarPos = propertiesDlg.ToolbarPosIndex;
				if (ToolBarPos == 3)
				{
					ToolBarPos = 4;
				}
				if (CoDisplay != 0)
				{
					codisp.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					componentNames.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else
				{
					codisp.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					componentNames.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				if (TransparentLabels != 0)
				{
					_pfsplot1.SetTextMode(1);
				}
				else
				{
					_pfsplot1.SetTextMode(0);
				}
				Properties.PFont.PName = propertiesDlg.LabelFont;
				Properties.PFont.PSize = propertiesDlg.LabelSize;
				Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour = (TPColour)propertiesDlg.CurveRisidueColour;
				Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour = (TPColour)propertiesDlg.CurveDistillationColour;
				Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propertiesDlg.CurveResidueMarkerStyleIndex;
				Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propertiesDlg.CurveDistillationMarkerStyleIndex;
				Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize = (int)propertiesDlg.CurveResidueMarkerSizeValue;
				Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize = (int)propertiesDlg.CurveDistillationMarkerSizeValue;
				Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine = (TPLine)propertiesDlg.CurveResidueLineStyleIndex;
				Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine = (TPLine)propertiesDlg.CurveDistillationLineStyleIndex;
				Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour = (TPColour)propertiesDlg.MarkerAzeoColour;
				Properties.PUserMarker.PColour = (TPColour)propertiesDlg.MarkerUserColour;
				Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propertiesDlg.MarkerAzeoMarkerStyleIndex;
				Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propertiesDlg.MarkerUserMarkerStyleIndex;
				Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize = (int)propertiesDlg.MarkerAzeoSizeValue;
				Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize = (int)propertiesDlg.MarkerUserSizeValue;
				Properties.PTieLine.PColour = (TPColour)propertiesDlg.LineTieColour;
				Properties.PUserLine.PColour = (TPColour)propertiesDlg.LineUserColour;

		{

			((base.Resources["mnuMarker"] as ContextMenu).Items[3] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
		}

		{

			((base.Resources["mnuMarker"] as ContextMenu).Items[3] as MenuItem).IsEnabled = true;
		}

			}
		}

	public class UserCompositionGrid : UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		public delegate void UpdateStatusEventHandler(object source, UpdateStatusEventArgs e);

		public delegate void InPropgressEventHanlder(object source, InPropgressEventArgs e);

		private bool _hasLoaded;

		private ISplitDataObj _objDataClass;

		private DisplayType m_nDisplayType = DisplayType.DT_COMPACT;

		private object[] m_DataArray;

		private string _tempUnit = "C";

		private BasisType cgBasis;

		private int m_nRows;

		private int m_nCols;

		internal ToolBar toolbar1;

		internal Button btnCopy;

		internal Button btnTemperature;

		internal Popup menuTemperature;

		internal ListBox lstTemperature;

		internal Button btnBasis;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Image imgBasis;

		internal Popup menuBasis;

		internal ListBox lstBasis;

		internal XamDataGrid igGrid;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public ISplitDataObj m_objDataClass
		{
			get
			{
				return _objDataClass;
			}
			set
			{
				_objDataClass = value;
				_objDataClass.m_bInputChanged = true;
			}
		}

		public GridType GridType { get; set; }

		public event UpdateStatusEventHandler UpdateStatusBar;

		public event InPropgressEventHanlder InProgress;

		public UserCompositionGrid()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.Loaded += UserCompositionGrid_Loaded;
		}

		private void UserCompositionGrid_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				lstTemperature.Items.Clear();
				string[] gTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits;
				foreach (string content in gTempUnits)
				{
					lstTemperature.Items.Add(new ListBoxItem
					{
						Content = content
					});
				}
				(lstTemperature.Items[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef] as ListBoxItem).IsEnabled = false;
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
		}

		private void btnTemperature_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			menuTemperature.IsOpen = true;
		}

		private void lstTemperature_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			menuTemperature.IsOpen = false;
			foreach (ListBoxItem item in (IEnumerable)lstTemperature.Items)
			{
				item.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			(lstTemperature.SelectedItem as ListBoxItem).IsEnabled = false;
			_tempUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[lstTemperature.SelectedIndex];
			if ((m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() != 1) ? LoadDistillationMapData() : LoadAzeotropeAnalyisData())
			{
				DisplayGridData();
			}
		}

		private void lstBasis_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			menuBasis.IsOpen = false;
			foreach (ListBoxItem item in (IEnumerable)lstBasis.Items)
			{
				item.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			(lstBasis.SelectedItem as ListBoxItem).IsEnabled = false;
			if (lstBasis.SelectedIndex == 0)
			{
				imgBasis.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("basismole.png");
				cgBasis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
				btnBasis.ToolTip = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleFraction;
			}
			else
			{
				imgBasis.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("basismass.png");
				cgBasis = BasisType.BT_Mass;
				btnBasis.ToolTip = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassFraction;
			}
			if ((m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() != 1) ? LoadDistillationMapData() : LoadAzeotropeAnalyisData())
			{
				DisplayGridData();
			}
		}

		private void btnBasis_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			menuBasis.IsOpen = true;
		}

		private void btnCopy_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)igGrid).SelectedItems.Records).Count == 0)
			{
				((DataPresenterBase)igGrid).SelectedItems.Records.AddRange(((IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)igGrid).Records).ToArray());
				((DataPresenterBase)igGrid).ExecuteCommand(DataPresenterCommands.Copy);
				((DataPresenterBase)igGrid).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
			}
			else
			{
				((DataPresenterBase)igGrid).ExecuteCommand(DataPresenterCommands.Copy);
			}
		}

		private void btnPrint_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Infragistics.Windows.Reporting.Report report = new Infragistics.Windows.Reporting.Report();
			report.Sections.Add(new EmbeddedVisualReportSection((Visual)(object)igGrid));
			report.Print();
		}

		public bool ConfigureGrid(GridType nGridType)
		{
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ConfigResultGrid));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: true));
			Cursor overrideCursor = Mouse.OverrideCursor;
			try
			{
				Mouse.OverrideCursor = Cursors.Wait;
				GridType = nGridType;
				m_nDisplayType = DisplayType.DT_COMPACT;
				if (GridType == GridType.GT_Pure)
				{
					btnBasis.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				else
				{
					btnBasis.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				cgBasis = (BasisType)m_objDataClass.m_nBasis;
				if (cgBasis == BasisType.BT_Mole)
				{
					imgBasis.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("basismole.png");
					(lstBasis.Items[0] as ListBoxItem).IsEnabled = false;
					(lstBasis.Items[1] as ListBoxItem).IsEnabled = true;
					lstBasis.SelectedIndex = 0;
					btnBasis.ToolTip = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleFraction;
				}
				else
				{
					imgBasis.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("basismass.png");
					(lstBasis.Items[0] as ListBoxItem).IsEnabled = true;
					(lstBasis.Items[1] as ListBoxItem).IsEnabled = false;
					lstBasis.SelectedIndex = 1;
					btnBasis.ToolTip = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassFraction;
				}
				bool flag = ((m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() != 1) ? LoadDistillationMapData() : LoadAzeotropeAnalyisData());
				if (!flag)
				{
					m_DataArray = null;
				}
				DisplayGridData();
				this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
				this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
				return flag;
			}
			finally
			{
				Mouse.OverrideCursor = overrideCursor;
			}
		}

		private void DisplayGridData()
		{
			LoadGridHeaders();
			((DataPresenterBase)igGrid).DataSource = m_DataArray;
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
		}

		private void LoadGridHeaders()
		{
			switch (GridType)
			{
			case GridType.GT_Pure:
				LoadPureGridHeaders();
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Azeo:
			case GridType.GT_Both:
				LoadOtherGridHeaders();
				break;
			}
		}

		private bool LoadGridData(int nRows, double[] vTemp, double[,] vComp, int[] vStab, int nMix = 0, int[] vPhase = null)
		{
			string[] array;
			double[] MolecWtArray;
			if (m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() == 1)
			{
				array = (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).m_vCompIDs;
				MolecWtArray = (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).m_vCompMole;
			}
			else
			{
				array = (m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap).m_vPureCompNames;
				MolecWtArray = (m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap).m_vPureMole;
			}
			object[] dataArray;
			if (GridType == GridType.GT_Pure)
			{
				List<PureGridItem> list = new List<PureGridItem>();
				for (int i = 0; i < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; i++)
				{
					list.Add(new PureGridItem
					{
						Id = i + 1,
						Item1 = array[i],
						Item2 = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTempDouble(vTemp[i], _tempUnit),
						Item3 = AzeoGUIGlobals.GetStabilityString(vStab[i])
					});
				}
				dataArray = list.ToArray();
				m_DataArray = dataArray;
				return true;
			}
			List<AzeoGridItem> list2 = new List<AzeoGridItem>();
			for (int j = 0; j < nRows; j++)
			{
				AzeoGridItem azeoGridItem = new AzeoGridItem
				{
					Id = j + 1,
					Item1 = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTempDouble(vTemp[j], _tempUnit),
					Item2 = AzeoGUIGlobals.GetStabilityString(vStab[j]),
					Item3 = ((vPhase[j] == 1) ? WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Homogeneous : WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Heterogeneous)
				};
				int num = 0;
				double[] Composition_Array = new double[m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
				for (int k = 0; k < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; k++)
				{
					Composition_Array[k] = vComp[j, k];
					if (Composition_Array[k] != 0.0)
					{
						num++;
					}
				}
				azeoGridItem.Item4 = num.ToString();
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref MolecWtArray, cgBasis, m_objDataClass.m_nComp);
				for (int l = 0; l < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; l++)
				{
					azeoGridItem.GetType().GetProperty($"Item{l + 5}").SetValue(azeoGridItem, Composition_Array[l], null);
				}
				list2.Add(azeoGridItem);
			}
			dataArray = list2.ToArray();
			m_DataArray = dataArray;
			return true;
		}

		private bool LoadDistillationMapData()
		{
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InitializingPropertyCalculation));
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GeneratingResults));
			CDistillationMap cDistillationMap = m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap;
			if (cDistillationMap.GenerateTernaryMap() != 1)
			{
				this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
				return false;
			}
			if (cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes == 0 && GridType == GridType.GT_Azeo)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.NoAzeotrope);
				return false;
			}
			switch (GridType)
			{
			case GridType.GT_Azeo:
				m_nRows = cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes;
				m_nCols = 5 + cDistillationMap.m_nComp;
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Pure:
				m_nRows = 3;
				m_nCols = 4;
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Both:
				m_nRows = cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3;
				m_nCols = 5 + cDistillationMap.m_nComp;
				break;
			}
			if (m_nRows == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.LoadingData));
			bool result = false;
			switch (GridType)
			{
			case GridType.GT_Azeo:
				result = LoadGridData(cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes, cDistillationMap.m_vAzeoTemps, cDistillationMap.m_vAzeotropes, cDistillationMap.m_vAzeoStability, 0, cDistillationMap.m_vAzeoPhase);
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Pure:
				result = LoadGridData(3, cDistillationMap.m_vPureTemps, cDistillationMap.m_vPureComp, cDistillationMap.m_vPureStability);
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Both:
			{
				double[] vAzeoTemps = cDistillationMap.m_vAzeoTemps;
				int[] vAzeoStability = cDistillationMap.m_vAzeoStability;
				int[] vAzeoPhase = cDistillationMap.m_vAzeoPhase;
				double[] vPureTemps = cDistillationMap.m_vPureTemps;
				int[] vPureStability = cDistillationMap.m_vPureStability;
				double[,] vAzeotropes = cDistillationMap.m_vAzeotropes;
				double[] array = new double[cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3];
				int[] array2 = new int[cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3];
				int[] array3 = new int[cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3];
				double[,] array4 = new double[cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3, cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3];
				for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
				{
					array[i] = vPureTemps[i];
					array2[i] = vPureStability[i];
					array3[i] = 1;
				}
				for (int j = 3; j < cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3; j++)
				{
					array[j] = vAzeoTemps[j - 3];
					array2[j] = vAzeoStability[j - 3];
					array3[j] = vAzeoPhase[j - 3];
				}
				for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
				{
					for (int l = 0; l < 3; l++)
					{
						if (k == l)
						{
							array4[k, l] = 1.0;
						}
						else
						{
							array4[k, l] = 0.0;
						}
					}
				}
				for (int m = 3; m < cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3; m++)
				{
					for (int n = 0; n < 3; n++)
					{
						array4[m, n] = vAzeotropes[m - 3, n];
					}
				}
				result = LoadGridData(cDistillationMap.m_nAzeotropes + 3, array, array4, array2, 0, array3);
				break;
			}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool LoadAzeotropeAnalyisData()
		{
			//IL_005c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GeneratingResults));
			if ((m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).GetAzeotropes() != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes == 0 && GridType == GridType.GT_Azeo)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.NoAzeotrope, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.AzeotropeAnalysis, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return false;
			}
			switch (GridType)
			{
			case GridType.GT_Azeo:
				m_nRows = m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes;
				m_nCols = 5 + m_objDataClass.m_nComp;
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Pure:
				m_nRows = m_objDataClass.m_nComp;
				m_nCols = 4;
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Both:
				m_nRows = m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp;
				m_nCols = 5 + m_objDataClass.m_nComp;
				break;
			}
			if (m_nRows == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.LoadingData));
			bool result = false;
			switch (GridType)
			{
			case GridType.GT_Azeo:
				result = LoadGridData(m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes, m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoTemps, m_objDataClass.m_vAzeotropes, m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoStability, 0, m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoPhase);
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Pure:
				result = ((m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() != 1) ? LoadGridData(3, (m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap).m_vPureTemps, (m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap).m_vPureComp, (m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap).m_vPureStability) : LoadGridData(3, (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).m_vCompTemps, (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).m_vCompPoints, (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).m_vCompStability));
				break;
			case GridType.GT_Both:
			{
				double[] vAzeoTemps = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoTemps;
				int[] vAzeoStability = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoStability;
				int[] vAzeoPhase = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoPhase;
				double[] vCompTemps = (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).m_vCompTemps;
				int[] vCompStability = (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).m_vCompStability;
				double[,] vAzeotropes = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeotropes;
				double[] array = new double[m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
				int[] array2 = new int[m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
				int[] array3 = new int[m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
				double[,] array4 = new double[m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp, m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
				for (int i = 0; i < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; i++)
				{
					array[i] = vCompTemps[i];
					array2[i] = vCompStability[i];
					array3[i] = 1;
				}
				for (int j = m_objDataClass.m_nComp; j < m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp; j++)
				{
					array[j] = vAzeoTemps[j - m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
					array2[j] = vAzeoStability[j - m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
					array3[j] = vAzeoPhase[j - m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
				}
				for (int k = 0; k < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; k++)
				{
					for (int l = 0; l < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; l++)
					{
						if (k == l)
						{
							array4[k, l] = 1.0;
						}
						else
						{
							array4[k, l] = 0.0;
						}
					}
				}
				for (int m = m_objDataClass.m_nComp; m < m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp; m++)
				{
					for (int n = 0; n < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; n++)
					{
						array4[m, n] = vAzeotropes[m - m_objDataClass.m_nComp, n];
					}
				}
				result = LoadGridData(m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes + m_objDataClass.m_nComp, array, array4, array2, 0, array3);
				break;
			}
			}
			return result;
		}
