	public class UserTernaryPlot : UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private bool _hasLoaded;

		internal ToolBarTray topToolbarTray;

		internal TriPlot triPlot1;

		internal ToolBarTray rightToolbarTray;

		internal ToolBar toolbarPlot;

		internal ToggleButton tbBtnTieLine;

		internal Button tbBtnTieLineValue;

		internal ToggleButton tbBtnCurve;

		internal Button tbBtnCurveValue;

		internal ToggleButton tbBtnMarker;

		internal Button tbBtnMarkerValue;

		internal ToggleButton tbBtnDrawLine;

		internal Button tbBtnPrint;

		internal Button tbBtnCopy;

		internal Button tbBtnChangePlot;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Image tbBtnChangePlotImage;

		internal Button tbBtnRotate;

		internal Button tbBtnFlip;

		internal Button tbBtnZoomIn;

		internal Button tbBtnZoomOut;

		internal Button tbBtnZoomFull;

		internal ToggleButton tbBtnGridToggle;

		internal ToolBarTray bottomToolbarTray;

		public CDistillationMap DistillationMapObj { get; set; }

		public bool AddCurveOption
		{
			set
			{
				triPlot1.AddCurveOption = value;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<UpdateStatusEventArgs> UpdateStatusBar;

		public event EventHandler<InPropgressEventArgs> InProgress;

		public UserTernaryPlot()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.Loaded += UserTernaryPlot_Loaded;
			triPlot1.Title = "";
			triPlot1.Coordinates = CoordPair.XY;
			triPlot1.SetCoordinates();
			triPlot1.PlotType = PlotType.RightAngled;
			triPlot1.Grid = false;
			triPlot1.RegionDefined += triPlot1_RegionDefined;
			triPlot1.UpdatePlotTypeToolbar += triPlot1_UpdatePlotTypeToolbar;
			triPlot1.ZoomFull += triPlot1_ZoomFull;
			triPlot1.ZoomIn += triPlot1_ZoomIn;
			triPlot1.MouseStopped += triPlot1_MouseStopped;
			triPlot1.UnpressButton += triPlot1_UnpressButton;
			triPlot1.AddBoilingPoint += triPlot1_AddBoilingPoint;
			triPlot1.KeyUp += triPlot1_KeyUp;
			triPlot1.AddTieLine += triPlot1_AddTieLine;
			triPlot1.AddCurve += triPlot1_AddCurve;
			triPlot1.Relayout += triPlot1_Relayout;
		}

		public bool HasLoaded()
		{
			return _hasLoaded;
		}

		private void UserTernaryPlot_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				triPlot1.PlotZoomFull();
				tbBtnCurve.IsEnabled = triPlot1.AddCurveOption;
				tbBtnCurveValue.IsEnabled = triPlot1.AddCurveOption;
				triPlot1.PD_PressureValue = DistillationMapObj.m_dbPressure.ToString();
				triPlot1.PD_PressureUnits = DistillationMapObj.m_sPressureUnits;
				triPlot1.PD_ModelName = DistillationMapObj.m_szVLEModel;
				triPlot1.PD_LLEModelName = DistillationMapObj.m_szLLEModel;
				triPlot1.PD_TempValue = DistillationMapObj.m_dbTemp.ToString();
				triPlot1.TempUnits = DistillationMapObj.m_sTempUnits;
				triPlot1.PD_Phases = DistillationMapObj.m_nPhase.ToString();
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
		}

		public void FinishedAdding()
		{
			triPlot1.FinishedAdding();
			tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
		}

		public void SetToolbar(string name, bool status)
		{
			switch (name)
			{
			case "TieLine":
				tbBtnTieLine.IsEnabled = status;
				break;
			case "TieLineValue":
				tbBtnTieLineValue.IsEnabled = status;
				break;
			case "Marker":
				tbBtnMarker.IsEnabled = status;
				break;
			case "MarkerValue":
				tbBtnMarkerValue.IsEnabled = status;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void ForceRefresh()
		{
			triPlot1.Refresh();
		}

		public void FroceRedraw()
		{
			triPlot1.Redraw();
		}

		public int PlotMap()
		{
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			bool flag4 = false;
			ObjID objID = null;
			tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
			triPlot1.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			triPlot1.MousePointer(1);
			if (DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged || DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEverything || !DistillationMapObj.m_PlottedBefore)
			{
				if (DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEverything || !DistillationMapObj.m_PlottedBefore)
				{
					flag = true;
					flag2 = true;
					flag3 = true;
					flag4 = true;
					triPlot1.Clear();
					DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEverything = false;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int i = 0; i <= 8; i++)
					{
						if (!DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[i] || !DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[i])
						{
							continue;
						}
						switch ((chkView)i)
						{
						case chkView.CV_Azeotropes:
							do
							{
								objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.MarkAzeotrope);
								if (objID.Id != -1)
								{
									triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
								}
							}
							while (objID.Id != -1);
							triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkAzeotrope);
							do
							{
								objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.TextAzeotrope);
								if (objID.Id != -1)
								{
									triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
								}
							}
							while (objID.Id != -1);
							triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TextAzeotrope);
							flag = true;
							continue;
						case chkView.CV_DisBound:
							do
							{
								objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
								if (objID.Id != -1)
								{
									triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
								}
							}
							while (objID.Id != -1);
							flag2 = true;
							continue;
						case chkView.CV_IsovolCurve:
							break;
						default:
							continue;
						}
						do
						{
							objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.CurveIso);
							if (objID.Id != -1)
							{
								triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
							}
						}
						while (objID.Id != -1);
						flag3 = true;
					}
					if (DistillationMapObj.m_PressureChanged)
					{
						do
						{
							objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.CurveResidue);
							if (objID.Id != -1)
							{
								triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
							}
						}
						while (objID.Id != -1);
						do
						{
							objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.CurveDistill);
							if (objID.Id != -1)
							{
								triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
							}
						}
						while (objID.Id != -1);
						triPlot1.RemoveAllLabelsFromMarker();
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
						DistillationMapObj.m_PressureChanged = false;
					}
				}
				this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InitializingPropertyCalculation));
				switch (DistillationMapObj.GenerateTernaryMap())
				{
				case -1:
					base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
					this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
					this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.TernaryMapGenerationFailed);
					return -1;
				case 0:
					base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
					this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
					this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
					return 0;
				case 1:
					if (DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged || DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEverything || !DistillationMapObj.m_PlottedBefore)
					{
						DistillationMapObj.m_PlottedBefore = true;
						flag4 = true;
					}
					break;
				}
				base.Cursor = Cursors.Wait;
				if (!triPlot1.TempUnits.Equals(DistillationMapObj.m_sDisplayTempUnits) || triPlot1.BasisType != DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis)
				{
					triPlot1.TempUnits = DistillationMapObj.m_sDisplayTempUnits;
					bool bConvertBasis = false;
					if (triPlot1.BasisType != DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis)
					{
						triPlot1.BasisType = DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis;
						bConvertBasis = true;
					}
					triPlot1.Redraw(bConvertBasis);
				}
				if (flag4)
				{
					triPlot1.LabelX = DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[0];
					triPlot1.LabelY1 = DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[1];
					triPlot1.LabelY2 = DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[2];
					triPlot1.Coordinates = CoordPair.XY;
				}
				triPlot1.SetPureTemperatures(DistillationMapObj.m_vPureTemps[0], DistillationMapObj.m_vPureTemps[1], DistillationMapObj.m_vPureTemps[2], (int)triPlot1.Coordinates);
				triPlot1.SetCoordinates();
				triPlot1.Refresh();
				double[] array = new double[3];
				for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
				{
					array[j] = DistillationMapObj.GetMoleWt(DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[j]);
				}
				DistillationMapObj.m_vPureMole = array.Clone() as double[];
				triPlot1.Mol_Wt = array.Clone() as double[];
				if (DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[9])
				{
					if (DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[9])
					{
						objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.EnvLLE);
						if (objID.Id != -1)
						{
							triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
						}
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
						triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
						triPlot1.DisplayItem(state: true, ObjType.EnvLLE);
						this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculatingEnvelope));
						double num = AzeoGUIGlobals.KTemp(DistillationMapObj.m_dbTemp, DistillationMapObj.m_sTempUnits);
						if (num < 0.0)
						{
							AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.LLETempInvalid);
						}
						else
						{
							DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.Temperature = num;
							DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.Pressure = AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(DistillationMapObj.m_dbPressure, DistillationMapObj.m_sPressureUnits);
							try
							{
								DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.ComputeEnvelopeLLE();
								DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetCurveLLE(out var vXCurve);
								triPlot1.PlotEvenlope(vXCurve, ObjType.EnvLLE);
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
								AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculateLLEEvelopeFailed);
								goto IL_071a;
							}
							try
							{
								DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetTielinesLLE(out var vTieline);
								triPlot1.PlotTieLine(vTieline, ObjType.TieLineLLE);
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
								AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculateLLETieLinesFailed);
								goto IL_071a;
							}
							triPlot1.Redraw();
							DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[9] = false;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[9] = true;
				}
				goto IL_071a;
			}
			goto IL_0a17;
			IL_071a:
			if (DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[10])
			{
				if (DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[10])
				{
					objID = triPlot1.ObjectType(ObjType.EnvVLLE);
					if (objID.Id != -1)
					{
						triPlot1.DeleteItem(objID, force: true);
					}
					triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
					triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLE);
					triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
					triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
					triPlot1.RemoveFromArray(ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
					triPlot1.DisplayItem(state: true, ObjType.EnvVLLE);
					this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculatingEnvelope));
					DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.Pressure = AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(DistillationMapObj.m_dbPressure, DistillationMapObj.m_sPressureUnits);
					try
					{
						double dbPlaitTemp = 0.0;
						DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.ComputeEnvelopeVLLE();
						DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetCurveVLLE(out var vXCurve2, out dbPlaitTemp);
						DistillationMapObj.m_dbPlaitTemp = dbPlaitTemp;
						triPlot1.PlotEvenlope(vXCurve2, ObjType.EnvVLLE);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculateVLLEEvenlopeFailed);
						goto IL_08ad;
					}
					try
					{
						DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetTielinesVLLE(out var vTieline2, out var vTiePoints);
						triPlot1.PlotTieLine(vTieline2, ObjType.TieLineVLLE, vTiePoints);
						triPlot1.Redraw();
						DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[10] = false;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculateVLLETeLinesFailed);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[10] = true;
			}
			goto IL_08ad;
			IL_0a17:
			triPlot1.Refresh();
			base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
			DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEnvelope = false;
			DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcTernary = false;
			DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEverything = false;
			DistillationMapObj.m_PressureChanged = false;
			return 1;
			IL_08ad:
			for (int k = 0; k <= 10; k++)
			{
				if (!DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[k])
				{
					continue;
				}
				switch ((chkView)k)
				{
				case chkView.CV_Azeotropes:
					if (flag && DistillationMapObj.m_nAzeotropes > 0)
					{
						triPlot1.PlotAzeotropes(DistillationMapObj.m_vAzeotropes, DistillationMapObj.m_nAzeotropes, DistillationMapObj.m_vAzeoTemps);
					}
					break;
				case chkView.CV_DisBound:
					if (DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[0] && flag2)
					{
						triPlot1.PlotDistillationBoundary(DistillationMapObj.m_vCurve, DistillationMapObj.m_vCurveDir);
					}
					break;
				case chkView.CV_IsovolCurve:
					if (flag3 && DistillationMapObj.m_nIsoCurvePoints > 0)
					{
						triPlot1.PlotIsoCurve(DistillationMapObj.m_vIsoCurve);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.UpdatingPlot));
			if (!triPlot1.TempUnits.Equals(DistillationMapObj.m_sDisplayTempUnits) || triPlot1.BasisType != DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis)
			{
				triPlot1.TempUnits = DistillationMapObj.m_sDisplayTempUnits;
				triPlot1.BasisType = DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis;
				triPlot1.Redraw();
			}
			goto IL_0a17;
		}

		public void DisplayItem(bool state, ObjType what)
		{
			if (what == ObjType.CurveResidue || what == ObjType.CurveDistill)
			{
				tbBtnCurve.IsEnabled = state;
				tbBtnCurveValue.IsEnabled = state;
				if (!state)
				{
					tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
					triPlot1.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
					triPlot1.MousePointer(1);
				}
			}
			triPlot1.DisplayItem(state, what);
			triPlot1.Refresh();
		}

		private void triPlot1_RegionDefined(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			tbBtnZoomIn.IsEnabled = true;
		}

		private void triPlot1_UpdatePlotTypeToolbar(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			switch (triPlot1.PlotType)
			{
			case PlotType.Equilateral:
				tbBtnChangePlotImage.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("triright.ico");
				break;
			case PlotType.RightAngled:
				tbBtnChangePlotImage.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("3d.ico");
				break;
			}
		}

		private void triPlot1_ZoomIn(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			tbBtnZoomIn.IsEnabled = false;
			tbBtnZoomOut.IsEnabled = true;
			tbBtnZoomFull.IsEnabled = true;
		}

		private void triPlot1_ZoomFull(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			tbBtnZoomIn.IsEnabled = false;
			tbBtnZoomOut.IsEnabled = false;
			tbBtnZoomFull.IsEnabled = false;
		}

		private void triPlot1_MouseStopped(object sender, MouseStoppedEventArgs e)
		{
			double[] vComposition = new double[3]
			{
				e.X,
				e.Y,
				1.0 - e.X - e.Y
			};
			try
			{
				DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetBoilingPoint(vComposition, out var dbBoilingPoint);
				triPlot1.ShowBalloon(AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(dbBoilingPoint, triPlot1.TempUnits, showUnit: true));
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private void triPlot1_UnpressButton(object sender, UnpressButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Button)
			{
			case "TieLine":
				tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
				break;
			case "Marker":
				tbBtnMarker.IsChecked = false;
				break;
			case "DrawLine":
				tbBtnDrawLine.IsChecked = false;
				break;
			}
		}

		private void triPlot1_AddBoilingPoint(object sender, AddBoilingPointEventArgs e)
		{
			double[] vComposition = new double[3]
			{
				e.X,
				e.Y,
				1.0 - e.X - e.Y
			};
			try
			{
				DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetBoilingPoint(vComposition, out var dbBoilingPoint);
				triPlot1.MarkerText(AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(dbBoilingPoint, triPlot1.TempUnits, showUnit: true));
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
		}

		private void triPlot1_KeyUp(object sender, KeyUpEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.KeyCode == 27)
			{
				triPlot1.FinishedAdding();
				tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
				tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
			}
		}

		private void triPlot1_AddTieLine(object sender, AddTieLineArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0133: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculatingTieLine));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: true));
			double[] vComposition = new double[3]
			{
				e.X,
				e.Y,
				1.0 - e.X - e.Y
			};
			if (DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[10] || DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[9])
			{
				try
				{
					object vX = null;
					object vX2 = null;
					object vY = null;
					if (DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[10])
					{
						DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetNewTieLineVLLE(vComposition, out vX, out vX2, out vY);
					}
					else if (DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[9])
					{
						DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetNewTieLineLLE(vComposition, out vX, out vX2);
					}
					double[] array = vX as double[];
					double[] array2 = vX2 as double[];
					double[] array3 = vY as double[];
					if (array[0] == -999.0)
					{
						triPlot1.FinishedAdding();
						tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
						MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MixtureMiscible, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Mixture_Miscible_Title, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					}
					else
					{
						double[,] array4 = new double[2, 3];
						for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
						{
							array4[0, i] = array[i];
						}
						for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
						{
							array4[1, j] = array2[j];
						}
						if (DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[10])
						{
							double[,] array5 = new double[1, 3];
							for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
							{
								array5[0, k] = array3[k];
							}
							triPlot1.PlotTieLine(array4, ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE, array5);
						}
						else
						{
							triPlot1.PlotTieLine(array4, ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetNewLineFailed);
				}
			}
			triPlot1.Refresh();
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
		}

		private void triPlot1_AddCurve(object sender, AddCurveEventArgs e)
		{
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculatingCurve));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: true));
			double[] vInitPoint = new double[3] { e.X, e.Y, e.Z };
			int nDirection = 0;
			object vDistillationCurve = null;
			ObjType curveType = ObjType.None;
			if (DistillationMapObj.CurveType == Curve.ResidueCurve)
			{
				try
				{
					DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetResidueCurve(vInitPoint, out nDirection, out vDistillationCurve);
					curveType = ObjType.CurveResidue;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetResidueCurveFailed);
					this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
					this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				try
				{
					DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.GetDistillationCurve(vInitPoint, out nDirection, out vDistillationCurve);
					curveType = ObjType.CurveDistill;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetDisitillationCurveFailed);
					this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
					this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
				}
			}
			triPlot1.PlotCurve((double[,])vDistillationCurve, curveType, nDirection);
			triPlot1.Refresh();
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
		}

		private void triPlot1_Relayout(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			topToolbarTray.ToolBars.Clear();
			bottomToolbarTray.ToolBars.Clear();
			rightToolbarTray.ToolBars.Clear();
			switch (triPlot1.ToolBarPos)
			{
			case 4:
				rightToolbarTray.ToolBars.Add(toolbarPlot);
				break;
			case 1:
				topToolbarTray.ToolBars.Add(toolbarPlot);
				break;
			case 2:
				bottomToolbarTray.ToolBars.Add(toolbarPlot);
				break;
			case 0:
			case 3:
				break;
			}
		}

		private void tbBtnTieLine_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnMarker.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnDrawLine.IsChecked = false;
			if (tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked.HasValue && tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked.Value)
			{
				triPlot1.TieLineAdd_Start(bAddMany: true);
			}
			else
			{
				triPlot1.FinishedAdding();
			}
		}

		}

		private void tbBtnPrint_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnMarker.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnDrawLine.IsChecked = false;
			triPlot1.PD_PressureValue = DistillationMapObj.m_dbPressure.ToString();
			triPlot1.PD_PressureUnits = DistillationMapObj.m_sPressureUnits;
			triPlot1.PD_ModelName = DistillationMapObj.m_szVLEModel;
			triPlot1.PD_LLEModelName = DistillationMapObj.m_szLLEModel;
			triPlot1.PD_TempValue = DistillationMapObj.m_dbTemp.ToString();
			triPlot1.PD_TempUnits = DistillationMapObj.m_sTempUnits;
			triPlot1.PD_Phases = DistillationMapObj.m_nPhase.ToString();
			triPlot1.PrintPlot();
		}

		private void tbBtnCopy_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.Copy();
		}

		private void tbBtnChangePlot_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (triPlot1.PlotType == PlotType.RightAngled)
			{
				tbBtnChangePlotImage.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("triright.ico");
			}
			else
			{
				tbBtnChangePlotImage.Source = ImageResources.GetImageSource("3d.ico");
			}
			triPlot1.SwapPlotType();
		}

		private void tbBtnRotate_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.RotatePoints();
		}

		private void tbBtnFlip_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.FlipPoints();
		}

		private void tbBtnZoomIn_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnMarker.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnDrawLine.IsChecked = false;
			triPlot1.ZoomInRegion();
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ZoomedIn));
		}

		private void tbBtnZoomOut_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnMarker.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnDrawLine.IsChecked = false;
			triPlot1.ZoomOutRegion();
			if (triPlot1.ZoomCount == 1.0)
			{
				tbBtnZoomOut.IsEnabled = false;
				tbBtnZoomFull.IsEnabled = false;
				this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
			}
		}

		private void tbBtnZoomFull_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			tbBtnCurve.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnTieLine.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnMarker.IsChecked = false;
			tbBtnDrawLine.IsChecked = false;
			triPlot1.PlotZoomFull();
			tbBtnZoomOut.IsEnabled = false;
			tbBtnZoomFull.IsEnabled = false;
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
		}

		private void tbBtnGridToggle_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			triPlot1.Grid = tbBtnGridToggle.IsChecked.Value;
		}

		public UsrAzeoInput()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.Loaded += UsrAzeoInput_Loaded;
			txtPressure.PreviewKeyDown += txtPressure_PreviewKeyDown;
		}

		private void txtPressure_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			string systemDecimalSymbol = GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
			if (e.Key == Key.Decimal && systemDecimalSymbol == ",")
			{
				txtPressure.RaiseEvent(new TextCompositionEventArgs(e.Device, new TextComposition(InputManager.Current, txtPressure, ","))
				{
					RoutedEvent = UIElement.TextInputEvent
				});
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (e.Key == Key.OemPeriod && systemDecimalSymbol != ".")
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (e.Key == Key.OemComma && systemDecimalSymbol != ",")
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private static string GetSystemDecimalSymbol()
		{
			return 0.1.ToString().Replace("0", string.Empty).Replace("1", string.Empty);
		}

		private void UsrAzeoInput_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				base.DataContext = this;
				Phase = PhaseList[0];
				string[] gPressureUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureUnits;
				foreach (string item in gPressureUnits)
				{
					PresUnitsList.Add(item);
				}
				PresUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureDef];
				gPressureUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits;
				foreach (string item2 in gPressureUnits)
				{
					TempUnitsList.Add(item2);
				}
				TempUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
				Pressure = double.Parse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureValue);
				_hasLoaded = true;
				IsParentChecked = false;
			}
		}

		public void UpdateFormControl()
		{
			IHNodeCol iHNodeCol = null;
			IHNodeCol iHNodeCol2 = null;
			try
			{
				iHNodeCol = AzeoGUIGlobals.ComponentNode.Elements["ANAME1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements;
				iHNodeCol2 = AzeoGUIGlobals.ComponentNode.Elements["DBNAME1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			ComponentsList.Clear();
			if (iHNodeCol == null)
			{
				string[] array = new string[10];
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "ACETONE",
					Description = "ACETONE"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "METHANOL",
					Description = "METHANOL"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "CHFL",
					Description = "CHOROFORM"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "WATER",
					Description = "WATER"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "TOLUENE",
					Description = "TOLUENE"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "ACETACID",
					Description = "ACETIC ACID"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "MACETATE",
					Description = "METHYL ACETATE"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "BENZENE",
					Description = "BENZENE"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "ETHANOL",
					Description = "ETHANOL"
				});
				ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
				{
					IsSelected = false,
					Name = "EACETATE",
					Description = "ETHYL ACETATE"
				});
				array[0] = "Acetone";
				array[1] = "Methanol";
				array[2] = "Choroform";
				array[3] = "Water";
				array[4] = "Toluene";
				array[5] = "Acetic Acid";
				array[6] = "Methyl Acetate";
				array[7] = "Benzene";
				array[8] = "Ethanol";
				array[9] = "Ethyl Acetate";
				m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_sCompName = array;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] array = new string[iHNodeCol.Count];
				for (int i = 0; i < iHNodeCol.Count; i++)
				{
					string text = (dynamic)iHNodeCol2[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					ComponentsList.Add(new ComponentItem
					{
						IsSelected = false,
						Name = iHNodeCol.get_ItemName(i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing),
						Description = text
					});
					array[i] = text;
				}
				m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_sCompName = array;
			}
			LLEModelList.Clear();
			string lLEModel = null;
			IHNode iHNode = null;
			IHNodeCol iHNodeCol3 = null;
			try
			{
				lLEModel = (dynamic)AzeoGUIGlobals.PropertyNode.Elements["GOPSETNAME", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				iHNode = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Elements["Data", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Properties", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Property Methods", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing];
				if (iHNode != null)
				{
					iHNodeCol3 = iHNode.Elements;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			if (iHNodeCol3 == null)
			{
				LLEModelList.Add("NRTL-HOC");
				LLEModelList.Add("WILS-HOC");
				LLEModelList.Add("UNIQ-HOC");
				LLEModel = LLEModelList[0];
				return;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < iHNodeCol3.Count; j++)
			{
				string text2 = iHNodeCol3[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Name(Type.Missing);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					LLEModelList.Add(text2);
				}
			}
			LLEModel = lLEModel;
		}

		public bool Validate_Input()
		{
			//IL_0074: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0115: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_bInputChanged)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!double.TryParse(txtPressure.Text.Trim(), out var _))
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = ComponentsList.Count((ComponentItem p) => p.IsSelected);
			if (num < 2)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Select2Components, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				_Override = false;
				return false;
			}
			if (num > 100)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Select100Components, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				_Override = false;
				return false;
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			string[] array = new string[num];
			double[] array2 = new double[num];
			for (int num3 = 0; num3 < ComponentsList.Count; num3++)
			{
				ComponentItem componentItem = ComponentsList[num3];
				if (componentItem.IsSelected)
				{
					array[num2] = componentItem.Name;
					array2[num2] = num3;
					num2++;
				}
				if (num2 > 100)
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Select100Components, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					break;
				}
			}
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_vCompIDs = array;
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_nComp = num;
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_dbPressure = Pressure;
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_sPressureUnits = PresUnit;
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_szVLEModel = LLEModel;
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_sDisplayTempUnits = TempUnit;
			if (Phase == "VAP-LIQ")
			{
				m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_nPhase = 2;
			}
			else if (Phase == "VAP-LIQ-LIQ")
			{
				m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_nPhase = 3;
			}
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InitializingPropertyCalculation));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: true));
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculatingAzeotropes));
			try
			{
				if (m_objAzeotropeSearch.GetAzeotropes() != 1)
				{
					this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
					this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
					return false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculateAzeotropesFailed);
				_Override = false;
				return false;
			}
			double[] vMole = m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_vMole;
			for (int num4 = 0; num4 < num; num4++)
			{
				int num5 = Array.IndexOf(m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_szComp, array[num4]);
				if (num5 >= 0 && num5 < vMole.Length)
				{
					array2[num4] = vMole[num5];
				}
			}
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_vCompMole = array2;
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_nBasis = 2;
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_bInputChanged = false;
			return true;
		}

		private void txtPressure_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (e.Key == Key.Return && !double.TryParse(txtPressure.Text.Trim(), out var _))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidPressureValue, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private void txtPressure_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!double.TryParse(txtPressure.Text.Trim(), out var _))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidPressureValue, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private void CheckBox_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_bInputChanged = true;
			IsParentChecked = ComponentsList.Where((ComponentItem x) => x.IsSelected).Count() == ComponentsList.Count;
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		private void CheckBox_Click_1(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			m_objAzeotropeSearch.m_bInputChanged = true;
			if (!(e.OriginalSource is CheckBox checkBox))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ComponentItem components in ComponentsList)
			{
				components.IsSelected = checkBox.IsChecked.Value;
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				lvComponents = (ListView)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				((CheckBox)target).Click += CheckBox_Click_1;
				break;
			case 4:
				txtPressure = (TextBox)target;
				txtPressure.KeyUp += txtPressure_KeyUp;
				txtPressure.LostFocus += txtPressure_LostFocus;
				break;
			case 5:
				cboPresUnit = (ComboBox)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				cboLLEModel = (ComboBox)target;
				break;
			case 7:
				cboPhases = (ComboBox)target;
				break;
			case 8:
				cboTemperature = (ComboBox)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IStyleConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			if (connectionId == 3)
			{
				((CheckBox)target).Click += CheckBox_Click;
			}
		}
	}

		public bool GenerateReport()
		{
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GeneratingResults));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: true));
			if (m_objDataClass == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = 0;
			if (m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() == 2)
			{
				this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GeneratingSplitData));
				num = (m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap).GenerateTernaryMap();
			}
			else
			{
				this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CalculatingAzeotropes));
				num = (m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch).GetAzeotropes();
			}
			if (num != 1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int nComp = m_objDataClass.m_nComp;
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.FormattingReport));
			string[] array;
			double[] MolecWtArray;
			int[] array3;
			double[] array2;
			if (m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() == 1)
			{
				CAzeotropeSearch obj = m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch;
				array = obj.m_vCompIDs;
				MolecWtArray = obj.m_vCompMole;
				array2 = obj.m_vCompTemps;
				array3 = obj.m_vCompStability;
			}
			else
			{
				CDistillationMap obj2 = m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap;
				array = obj2.m_vPureCompNames;
				MolecWtArray = obj2.m_vPureMole;
				array2 = obj2.m_vPureTemps;
				array3 = obj2.m_vPureStability;
			}
			string[] szComp = m_objDataClass.m_szComp;
			int[] vAzeoStability = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoStability;
			string szVLEModel = m_objDataClass.m_szVLEModel;
			string[] sCompName = m_objDataClass.m_sCompName;
			string[] array4 = new string[m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
			if (sCompName != null && sCompName.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; i++)
				{
					_ = array[i].Length;
					int num2 = -1;
					do
					{
						num2++;
					}
					while (num2 < szComp.Length && i < array.Length && !szComp[num2].Equals(array[i]));
					if (i < array4.Length && num2 < sCompName.Length)
					{
						array4[i] = sCompName[num2];
					}
				}
			}
			string text = ((m_objDataClass.m_nPhase != 2) ? "VAP-LIQ-LIQ" : "VAP-LIQ");
			double[] array5 = new double[m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
			FileStream fileStream = null;
			try
			{
				fileStream = File.Open(ReportUri, FileMode.Create);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(ex.Message);
				return false;
			}
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<HTML>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<HEAD><meta http-equiv=\"Content-Type\" content=\"text/html; charset=UTF-8\" /></HEAD>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<BODY LINK=blue ALINK=yellow VLINK=#000055 BGCOLOR=white>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TABLE ALIGN=CENTER WIDTH=100% BORDER=0 CELLSPACING=0 CELLPADDING=0>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD ALIGN=CENTER BGCOLOR=#8811AA><FONT FACE=arial,helvetica SIZE=4 COLOR=white><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.AzeoropeSearchReportTitle + "</B></FONT></TD></TR>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "</TABLE>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<BR><FONT FACE=Helvetica>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TABLE ALIGN=CENTER CELLSPACING=10 CELLPADDING=5>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.PhysicalPropertyModel + "</B> " + szVLEModel + "</TD>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TD><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ValidPhase + "</B> " + text + "</TD></TR>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "</TABLE><BR>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<CENTER><FONT SIZE=4><B><I>" + string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MixtureInvestigated, m_objDataClass.m_dbPressure, m_objDataClass.m_sPressureUnits) + "</I></B></CENTER><BR>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TABLE BORDER=3 ALIGN=CENTER WIDTH=90%><TD>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TABLE BORDER=0 BGCOLOR=#EEEEEE WIDTH=100%>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD BGCOLOR=#AAAAAA WIDTH=25% ALIGN=CENTER><H5><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CompID + "</B></TD><TD BGCOLOR=#AAAAAA WIDTH=35% ALIGN=CENTER><H5><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CompName + "</B></TD><TD BGCOLOR=#AAAAAA WIDTH=25% ALIGN=CENTER><H5><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Classification + "</B></TD><TD BGCOLOR=#AAAAAA WIDTH=15% ALIGN=CENTER><H5><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Header_Temperature + "</B></TD></TR>");
			for (int j = 0; j < nComp; j++)
			{
				string stabilityString = AzeoGUIGlobals.GetStabilityString(array3[j]);
				string text2 = ((array4 == null || array4.Length == 0) ? WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.NotAvailable : array4[j]);
				AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD><H5>" + array[j] + "</TD><TD VALIGN=MIDDLE><H5>" + text2 + "</TD><TD ALIGN=CENTER VALIGN=MIDDLE><H5>" + stabilityString + "</TD><TD ALIGN=CENTER VALIGN=MIDDLE><H5>" + AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(array2[j], m_objDataClass.m_sDisplayTempUnits, showUnit: true) + "</TR>");
			}
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "</TABLE></TD></TABLE>");
			if (m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes > 0)
			{
				double[,] vAzeotropes = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeotropes;
				AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<BR><BR>");
				if (m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes > 1)
				{
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<CENTER><FONT SIZE=4><B><I>" + string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SomeAzeotropesFound, m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes) + "</I></B></FONT></CENTER><BR>");
				}
				else
				{
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<CENTER><FONT SIZE=4><B><I>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.OneAzeotropeFound + "</I></B></FONT></CENTER><BR>");
				}
				array2 = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoTemps;
				int[] vAzeoPhase = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoPhase;
				for (int k = 0; k < m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes; k++)
				{
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TABLE BORDER=3 WIDTH=90% ALIGN=CENTER>");
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD BGCOLOR=#8811AA ALIGN=CENTER><FONT FACE=arial,helvetica SIZE=4 COLOR=#FFFFFF>" + $"{k + 1:00}" + "</FONT></TD><TD>");
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TABLE BORDER=0 BGCOLOR=#EEEEEE WIDTH=100% CELLSPACING=0>");
					for (int l = 0; l < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; l++)
					{
						array5[l] = vAzeotropes[k, l];
						Composition_Array[l] = vAzeotropes[k, l];
					}
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref MolecWtArray, BasisType.BT_Mass, m_objDataClass.m_nComp);
					int num3 = 0;
					for (int m = 0; m < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; m++)
					{
						if (array5[m] != 0.0)
						{
							num3++;
						}
					}
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD><H5><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ComponentsNumber + "</B> " + num3 + "</TD><TD COLSPAN=2 ALIGN=CENTER><H5>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Header_Temperature + " " + AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(array2[k], m_objDataClass.m_sDisplayTempUnits, showUnit: true) + "</TD></TR>");
					string text3 = ((vAzeoPhase[k] != 1) ? WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Heterogeneous : WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Homogeneous);
					string stabilityString = AzeoGUIGlobals.GetStabilityString(vAzeoStability[k]);
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD><H5><B>" + text3 + "</B></TD><TD COLSPAN=2 ALIGN=CENTER><H5><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Classification + ": " + stabilityString + "</B></TD></TR>");
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD BGCOLOR=#AAAAAA> </TD><TD VALIGN=BOTTOM BGCOLOR=#AAAAAA><H5 ALIGN=CENTER><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleBasis.ToUpper() + "</B></TD><TD ALIGN=CENTER VALIGN=BOTTOM BGCOLOR=#AAAAAA><H5><B>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassBasis.ToUpper() + "</B></TD></TR>");
					for (int n = 0; n < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; n++)
					{
						if (array5[n] != 0.0)
						{
							AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<TR><TD ALIGN=RIGHT VALIGNMENT=MIDDLE><H5>" + array[n] + "</TD><TD ALIGN=CENTER VALIGN=MIDDLE>" + $"{array5[n]:0.0000}" + "</TD><TD ALIGN=CENTER VALIGN=MIDDLE>" + $"{Composition_Array[n]: 0.0000}" + "</TD></TR>");
						}
					}
					AddLineToFile(fileStream, "</TABLE></TD></TR></TABLE><BR>");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<CENTER><FONT SIZE=4><B><I>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.NoAzeotropeFound + "</I></B></FONT></CENTER><BR>");
			}
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "</FONT>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "<H6 ALIGN=RIGHT><HR SIZE=2 COLOR=#330066>" + WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CompanyInfo + "</H6>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "</BODY>");
			AddLineToFile(fileStream, "</HTML>");
			fileStream.Close();
			fileStream.Dispose();
			webBrowser1.Navigate(ReportUri);
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
			this.InProgress(this, new InPropgressEventArgs(running: false));
			return true;
		}

		private void btnPrint_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (webBrowser1.Document is IHTMLDocument2 iHTMLDocument)
			{
				iHTMLDocument.execCommand("Print", showUI: true);
			}
		}

		private void btnCopy_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (webBrowser1.Document is IHTMLDocument2 { selection: { } selection } && selection.createRange() is IHTMLTxtRange { text: not null } iHTMLTxtRange)
			{
				ClipboardWrapper.SetDataObject(iHTMLTxtRange.text);
			}
		}

		private void btnSave_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_012e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			SaveFileDialog saveFileDialog = new SaveFileDialog();
			saveFileDialog.RestoreDirectory = true;
			saveFileDialog.Filter = "HTML file (*.html)|*.html|Text file (*.txt)|*.txt|PDF file (*.pdf)|*.pdf";
			if (saveFileDialog.ShowDialog() != true)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				string fileName = saveFileDialog.FileName;
				switch (System.IO.Path.GetExtension(fileName).ToLower())
				{
				case ".html":
					File.Copy(ReportUri, fileName, overwrite: true);
					break;
				case ".txt":
				{
					if (!(webBrowser1.Document is IHTMLDocument2 iHTMLDocument))
					{
						break;
					}
					iHTMLDocument.execCommand("SelectAll");
					IHTMLSelectionObject selection = iHTMLDocument.selection;
					if (selection != null && selection.createRange() is IHTMLTxtRange { text: not null } iHTMLTxtRange)
					{
						_ = iHTMLTxtRange.text;
						iHTMLDocument.execCommand("Unselect");
						if (File.Exists(fileName))
						{
							File.Delete(fileName);
						}
						File.AppendAllText(fileName, iHTMLTxtRange.text);
					}
					break;
				}
				case ".pdf":
					GeneratePdfReport(fileName);
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "", (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private void GeneratePdfReport(string fileName)
		{
			int nComp = m_objDataClass.m_nComp;
			string[] array;
			double[] MolecWtArray;
			int[] array3;
			double[] array2;
			if (m_objDataClass.WhatAmI() == 1)
			{
				CAzeotropeSearch obj = m_objDataClass as CAzeotropeSearch;
				array = obj.m_vCompIDs;
				MolecWtArray = obj.m_vCompMole;
				array2 = obj.m_vCompTemps;
				array3 = obj.m_vCompStability;
			}
			else
			{
				CDistillationMap obj2 = m_objDataClass as CDistillationMap;
				array = obj2.m_vPureCompNames;
				MolecWtArray = obj2.m_vPureMole;
				array2 = obj2.m_vPureTemps;
				array3 = obj2.m_vPureStability;
			}
			string[] szComp = m_objDataClass.m_szComp;
			int[] vAzeoStability = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoStability;
			string szVLEModel = m_objDataClass.m_szVLEModel;
			string[] sCompName = m_objDataClass.m_sCompName;
			string[] array4 = new string[m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
			if (sCompName != null && sCompName.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; i++)
				{
					_ = array[i].Length;
					int num = -1;
					do
					{
						num++;
					}
					while (num < szComp.Length && i < array.Length && !szComp[num].Equals(array[i]));
					if (i < array4.Length && num < sCompName.Length)
					{
						array4[i] = sCompName[num];
					}
				}
			}
			string text = ((m_objDataClass.m_nPhase != 2) ? "VAP-LIQ-LIQ" : "VAP-LIQ");
			double[] array5 = new double[m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[m_objDataClass.m_nComp];
			Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Report.Report report = new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Report.Report();
			ISection section = report.AddSection();
			section.PageSize = PageSizes.A4;
			section.PageMargins.All = 35f;
			IText text2 = section.AddText();
			text2.AddContent(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.AzeoropeSearchReportTitle, new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Report.Text.Style(new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Font("Arial", 18f, Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.FontStyle.Bold), Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Brushes.White));
			text2.Background = new Background(new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Color(136, 17, 170));
			text2.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
			IText text3 = section.AddText();
			text3.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
			text3.AddLineBreak();
			text3.AddLineBreak();
			text3.AddContent(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.PhysicalPropertyModel, TextStyle_Bold);
			text3.AddContent(szVLEModel);
			text3.AddContent("  ");
			text3.AddContent(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ValidPhase, TextStyle_Bold);
			text3.AddContent(text);
			IText text4 = section.AddText();
			text4.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
			text4.AddLineBreak();
			text4.AddLineBreak();
			text4.AddContent(string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MixtureInvestigated, m_objDataClass.m_dbPressure, m_objDataClass.m_sPressureUnits), TextStyle_SubHeader);
			ITable table = section.AddTable();
			table.Background = new Background(DefaultTableGridColor);
			table.Margins = new Margins(10f);
			AddHeaderCell(table.Header, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CompID);
			AddHeaderCell(table.Header, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.CompName);
			AddHeaderCell(table.Header, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Classification);
			AddHeaderCell(table.Header, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Header_Temperature);
			for (int j = 0; j < nComp; j++)
			{
				string stabilityString = AzeoGUIGlobals.GetStabilityString(array3[j]);
				string s = ((array4 == null || array4.Length == 0) ? WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.NotAvailable : array4[j]);
				ITableRow row = table.AddRow();
				AddTableCell(row, array[j]);
				AddTableCell(row, s);
				AddTableCell(row, stabilityString, Alignment.Center);
				AddTableCell(row, AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(array2[j], m_objDataClass.m_sDisplayTempUnits, showUnit: true), Alignment.Center);
			}
			if (m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes > 0)
			{
				double[,] vAzeotropes = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeotropes;
				string text5 = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.OneAzeotropeFound;
				if (m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes > 1)
				{
					text5 = string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SomeAzeotropesFound, m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes);
				}
				IText text6 = section.AddText();
				text6.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
				text6.AddLineBreak();
				text6.AddLineBreak();
				text6.AddContent(text5, TextStyle_SubHeader);
				array2 = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoTemps;
				int[] vAzeoPhase = m_objDataClass.m_vAzeoPhase;
				for (int k = 0; k < m_objDataClass.m_nAzeotropes; k++)
				{
					table = section.AddTable();
					table.Background = new Background(DefaultTableGridColor);
					table.Margins = new Margins(10f);
					ITableRow tableRow = table.AddRow();
					ITableCell tableCell = tableRow.AddCell();
					tableCell.Width = new RelativeWidth(10f);
					tableCell.Alignment.Vertical = Alignment.Middle;
					tableCell.Borders = new Borders(Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Pens.Black);
					tableCell.Background = new Background(new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Color(136, 17, 170));
					IText text7 = tableCell.AddText();
					text7.AddContent($"{k + 1:00}", new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Report.Text.Style(new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Font("Arial", 18f, Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.FontStyle.Bold), Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Brushes.White));
					text7.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
					ITable table2 = tableRow.AddCell().AddTable();
					for (int l = 0; l < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; l++)
					{
						array5[l] = vAzeotropes[k, l];
						Composition_Array[l] = vAzeotropes[k, l];
					}
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref MolecWtArray, BasisType.BT_Mass, m_objDataClass.m_nComp);
					int num2 = 0;
					for (int m = 0; m < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; m++)
					{
						if (array5[m] != 0.0)
						{
							num2++;
						}
					}
					tableRow = table2.AddRow();
					AddTableCell(tableRow, $"{WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ComponentsNumber} {num2}");
					AddTableCell(tableRow, $"{WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Header_Temperature} {AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(array2[k], m_objDataClass.m_sDisplayTempUnits, showUnit: true)}");
					string s2 = ((vAzeoPhase[k] != 1) ? WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Heterogeneous : WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Homogeneous);
					string stabilityString = AzeoGUIGlobals.GetStabilityString(vAzeoStability[k]);
					tableRow = table2.AddRow();
					AddTableCell(tableRow, s2);
					AddTableCell(tableRow, $"{WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Classification}: {stabilityString}");
					tableRow = table2.AddRow();
					AddTableCell(tableRow, "").Background = new Background(DefaultTableHeaderColor);
					AddTableCell(tableRow, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleBasis.ToUpper(), Alignment.Center).Background = new Background(DefaultTableHeaderColor);
					AddTableCell(tableRow, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassBasis.ToUpper(), Alignment.Center).Background = new Background(DefaultTableHeaderColor);
					for (int n = 0; n < m_objDataClass.m_nComp; n++)
					{
						if (array5[n] != 0.0)
						{
							tableRow = table2.AddRow();
							AddTableCell(tableRow, array[n], Alignment.Right);
							AddTableCell(tableRow, $"{array5[n]:0.0000}", Alignment.Center);
							AddTableCell(tableRow, $"{Composition_Array[n]: 0.0000}", Alignment.Center);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IText text8 = section.AddText();
				text8.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
				text8.AddLineBreak();
				text8.AddLineBreak();
				text8.AddContent(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.NoAzeotropeFound, TextStyle_SubHeader);
			}
			ISectionFooter sectionFooter = section.AddFooter();
			sectionFooter.Height = 30f;
			sectionFooter.Repeat = true;
			ILine line = sectionFooter.Shapes.AddLine();
			line.Pen = new Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Pen(Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Colors.Black, 1f);
			line.X1 = 0f;
			line.Y1 = 0f;
			line.X2 = section.PageSize.Width - section.PageMargins.Left - section.PageMargins.Right;
			line.Y2 = 0f;
			IText text9 = sectionFooter.AddText(0f, 0f, 0f);
			text9.AddContent("Aspen Technology, Inc.");
			text9.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Right;
			report.Publish(fileName, FileFormat.PDF);
		}

		private ITableCell AddHeaderCell(ITableHeader header, string s)
		{
			ITableCell tableCell = header.AddCell();
			tableCell.Paddings.All = 3f;
			tableCell.Borders = new Borders(Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Pens.Black);
			tableCell.Background = new Background(DefaultTableHeaderColor);
			tableCell.Alignment.Vertical = Alignment.Middle;
			tableCell.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
			IText text = tableCell.AddText();
			text.AddContent((s != null) ? s : string.Empty);
			text.Alignment.Horizontal = Alignment.Center;
			return tableCell;
		}

		private ITableCell AddTableCell(ITableRow row, string s, Alignment horizontalAlignment = Alignment.Left)
		{
			ITableCell tableCell = row.AddCell();
			tableCell.Alignment.Vertical = Alignment.Middle;
			tableCell.Alignment.Horizontal = horizontalAlignment;
			tableCell.Borders = new Borders(Infragistics.Documents.Reports.Graphics.Pens.Black);
			tableCell.Paddings.All = 3f;
			IText text = tableCell.AddText();
			text.AddContent((s != null) ? s : string.Empty);
			text.Alignment.Horizontal = horizontalAlignment;
			return tableCell;
		}

		private void AddLineToFile(FileStream fs, string line)
		{
			byte[] bytes = new UTF8Encoding().GetBytes(line);
			fs.Write(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
		}

				break;
			}
		}

		public string Comp1
		{
			get
			{
				return _comp1;
			}
			set
			{
				_comp1 = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("Comp1");
				DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcTernary = true;
				DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEverything = true;
				for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i++)
				{
					DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[i] = true;
				}
				DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[0] = value;
				DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
			}
		}

		public double Pressure
		{
			get
			{
				return _pressure;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value > 0.0 && _pressure != value)
				{
					_pressure = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Pressure");
					DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
					DistillationMapObj.m_PressureChanged = true;
					DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcTernary = true;
					for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i++)
					{
						DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[i] = true;
					}
					DistillationMapObj.m_dbPressure = value;
					this.PressureChanged(this, new PressureChangedEventArgs(_pressure));
				}
			}
		}

		public string Phase
		{
			get
			{
				return _phase;
			}
			set
			{
				_phase = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("Phase");
				if (DistillationMapObj != null)
				{
					DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcTernary = true;
					DistillationMapObj.m_PressureChanged = true;
					DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
					for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i++)
					{
						DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[i] = true;
					}
					DistillationMapObj.m_nPhase = PhasesList.IndexOf(value) + 2;
				}
				if (value == "VAP-LIQ-LIQ")
				{
					this.NoEnvelopes(this, new NoEnvelopesEventArgs(_stat: false));
					chkVLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = true;
					chkLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					chkVLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = false;
					chkLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = false;
					this.NoEnvelopes(this, new NoEnvelopesEventArgs(_stat: true));
				}
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<string> PhasesList => _phasesList;

		public bool IsResidueCurveChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isResidueCurveChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isResidueCurveChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsResidueCurveChecked");
				this.CheckCurves(this, new CheckCurvesEventArgs(value));
				if (DistillationMapObj != null)
				{
					if (value)
					{
						chkDistillationCurve.IsEnabled = false;
						DistillationMapObj.CurveType = Curve.ResidueCurve;
						this.StopCurveMenu(this, new StopCurveMenuEventArgs(_mode: true));
					}
					else
					{
						chkDistillationCurve.IsEnabled = true;
						this.StopCurveMenu(this, new StopCurveMenuEventArgs(_mode: false));
					}
					DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsDistillationCurveChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isDistillationCurveChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isDistillationCurveChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsDistillationCurveChecked");
				this.CheckCurves(this, new CheckCurvesEventArgs(value));
				if (value)
				{
					chkResidueCurve.IsEnabled = false;
					if (DistillationMapObj != null)
					{
						DistillationMapObj.CurveType = Curve.DistillationCurve;
					}
					this.StopCurveMenu(this, new StopCurveMenuEventArgs(_mode: true));
				}
				else
				{
					chkResidueCurve.IsEnabled = true;
					this.StopCurveMenu(this, new StopCurveMenuEventArgs(_mode: false));
				}
				this.CheckCurves(this, new CheckCurvesEventArgs(value));
				DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
			}
		}

		public bool IsAzeotropesChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isAzeotropesChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isAzeotropesChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsAzeotropesChecked");
				if (DistillationMapObj == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value)
				{
					chkDistillation.IsEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					chkDistillation.IsEnabled = false;
					IsDistillationChecked = false;
					try
					{
						DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.CurveCalcOption = 2;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				this.CheckAzeotropes(this, new CheckAzeotropesEventArgs(value));
				DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
			}
		}

		public bool IsDistillationChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isDistillationChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isDistillationChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsDistillationChecked");
				DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
				this.CheckDistillBound(this, new CheckDistillBoundEventArgs(value));
			}
		}

		public bool IsVLLEnvelopeChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isVLLEnvelopeChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isVLLEnvelopeChecked == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isVLLEnvelopeChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsVLLEnvelopeChecked");
				if (DistillationMapObj != null)
				{
					DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
					this.CheckLLE(this, new CheckLLEEventArgs(value));
					if (value)
					{
						chkLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = false;
						txtLLTemp.IsEnabled = false;
						cboLLTempUnits.IsEnabled = false;
						DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcTernary = true;
					}
					else
					{
						chkLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = true;
						txtLLTemp.IsEnabled = false;
						cboLLTempUnits.IsEnabled = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsLLEEnvelopeChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isLLEEnvelopeChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isLLEEnvelopeChecked == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_isLLEEnvelopeChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsLLEEnvelopeChecked");
				if (DistillationMapObj == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
				this.CheckVLLE(this, new CheckVLLEventArgs(value));
				if (value)
				{
					chkVLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = false;
					txtLLTemp.IsEnabled = true;
					cboLLTempUnits.IsEnabled = true;
					DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcTernary = true;
					txtLLTemp.Focus();
					return;
				}
				chkVLLEnvelope.IsEnabled = true;
				txtLLTemp.IsEnabled = false;
				cboLLTempUnits.IsEnabled = false;
				if (!IsVLLEnvelopeChecked)
				{
					this.NoEnvelopes(this, new NoEnvelopesEventArgs(_stat: true));
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsIsovolatilityChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isIsovolatilityChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isIsovolatilityChecked = value;
				DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;

			}
		}

					DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEnvelope = true;
					DistillationMapObj.m_dbTemp = value;

				}
			}
		}

						DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[9] = true;
					}
				}
			}
		}

			base.Loaded += UsrTernaryInput_Loaded;
		}

		private void UsrTernaryInput_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_hasLoaded)
			{
				base.DataContext = this;
				PhasesList.Clear();
				PhasesList.Add("VAP-LIQ");
				PhasesList.Add("VAP-LIQ-LIQ");
				Phase = PhasesList[1];
				PresUnitsList.Clear();
				string[] gPressureUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureUnits;
				foreach (string item in gPressureUnits)
				{
					PresUnitsList.Add(item);
				}
				TempUnitsList.Clear();
				gPressureUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits;
				foreach (string item2 in gPressureUnits)
				{
					TempUnitsList.Add(item2);
				}
				LLTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
				SelectedTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
				LLTemp = double.Parse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempValue);
				PlaitTemp = 0.0;
				PresUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureDef];
				Pressure = double.Parse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureValue);
				IsResidueCurveChecked = true;
				IsAzeotropesChecked = true;
				IsVLLEnvelopeChecked = true;
				IsDistillationChecked = true;
				_hasLoaded = true;
			}
		}

		public void UpdateFormControl()
		{
			IHNodeCol iHNodeCol = null;
			IHNodeCol iHNodeCol2 = null;
			try
			{
				iHNodeCol = AzeoGUIGlobals.ComponentNode.Elements["ANAME1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements;
				iHNodeCol2 = AzeoGUIGlobals.ComponentNode.Elements["DBNAME1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			ComponentsList.Clear();
			if (iHNodeCol == null)
			{
				string[] array = new string[10];
				ComponentsList.Add("WATER");
				ComponentsList.Add("CHFL");
				ComponentsList.Add("ACETACID");
				ComponentsList.Add("MACETATE");
				ComponentsList.Add("BENZENE");
				ComponentsList.Add("ACETONE");
				ComponentsList.Add("METHANOL");
				ComponentsList.Add("BZ");
				ComponentsList.Add("C1");
				ComponentsList.Add("CH");
				ComponentsList.Add("H2");
				ComponentsList.Add("N2");
				Comp1 = "ACETONE";
				Comp2 = "METHANOL";
				Comp3 = "CHFL";
				array[0] = "Acetone";
				array[1] = "Methanol";
				array[2] = "Choroform";
				array[3] = "Water";
				array[4] = "Toluene";
				array[5] = "Acetic Acid";
				array[6] = "Methyl Acetate";
				array[7] = "Benzene";
				array[8] = "Ethanol";
				array[9] = "Ethyl Acetate";
				DistillationMapObj.m_sCompName = array;
			}
			else
			{
				ComponentsList.Clear();
				string[] array = new string[iHNodeCol.Count];
				for (int i = 0; i < iHNodeCol.Count; i++)
				{
					ComponentsList.Add(iHNodeCol.get_ItemName(i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing));
					array[i] = (dynamic)iHNodeCol2[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				}
				DistillationMapObj.m_sCompName = array;
				Comp1 = ComponentsList[0];
				Comp2 = ComponentsList[1];
				Comp3 = ComponentsList[2];
			}
			VLEModelList.Clear();
			LLEModelList.Clear();
			string text = null;
			IHNode iHNode = null;
			IHNodeCol iHNodeCol3 = null;
			try
			{
				text = (dynamic)AzeoGUIGlobals.PropertyNode.Elements["GOPSETNAME", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				iHNode = AzeoGUIGlobals.IAspenPlusRootNode.Elements["Data", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Properties", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["Property Methods", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing];
				if (iHNode != null)
				{
					iHNodeCol3 = iHNode.Elements;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			if (iHNodeCol3 == null)
			{
				LLEModelList.Add("NRTL-HOC");
				LLEModelList.Add("WILS-HOC");
				LLEModelList.Add("UNIQ-HOC");
				VLEModelList.Add("NRTL-HOC");
				VLEModelList.Add("WILS-HOC");
				VLEModelList.Add("UNIQ-HOC");
				LLEModel = LLEModelList[0];
				VLEModel = VLEModelList[0];
			}
			else
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < iHNodeCol3.Count; j++)
				{
					string text2 = iHNodeCol3[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Name(Type.Missing);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						LLEModelList.Add(text2);
						VLEModelList.Add(text2);
					}
				}
				LLEModel = text;
				VLEModel = text;
			}
			LLTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
			SelectedTempUnits = AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempDef];
			LLTemp = double.Parse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gTempValue);
			PresUnit = AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureUnits[AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureDef];
			Pressure = double.Parse(AzeoGUIGlobals.gPressureValue);
			radioMoleFraction.IsChecked = true;
			DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis = 2;
		}

		public bool Validate_Input()
		{
			//IL_00c4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01ca: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged)
			{
				return true;
			}
			DistillationMapObj.m_sTempUnits = LLTempUnits;
			if (IsLLEEnvelopeChecked && !double.TryParse(txtLLTemp.Text, out var _))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidTemperatureValue, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return false;
			}
			DistillationMapObj.m_dbTemp = LLTemp;
			if (Comp1.Equals(Comp2) || Comp1.Equals(Comp3) || Comp2.Equals(Comp3))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SpecifyUniqueComponents, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
				return false;
			}
			DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[0] = Comp1;
			DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[1] = Comp2;
			DistillationMapObj.m_vPureCompNames[2] = Comp3;
			DistillationMapObj.m_nComp = 3;
			DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[10] = IsVLLEnvelopeChecked;
			DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[9] = IsLLEEnvelopeChecked;
			DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[3] = IsDistillationCurveChecked;
			DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[1] = IsDistillationChecked;
			DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[0] = IsAzeotropesChecked;
			DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[2] = IsIsovolatilityChecked;
			if (!double.TryParse(txtPressure.Text, out var result2))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidPressureValue, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return false;
			}
			DistillationMapObj.m_dbPressure = result2;
			DistillationMapObj.m_sPressureUnits = PresUnit;
			DistillationMapObj.m_szLLEModel = LLEModel;
			DistillationMapObj.m_szVLEModel = VLEModel;
			if (Phase == "VAP-LIQ")
			{
				DistillationMapObj.m_nPhase = 2;
				DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[9] = false;
				DistillationMapObj.m_CalcThis[10] = false;
			}
			else
			{
				DistillationMapObj.m_nPhase = 3;
			}
			this.UpdateStatusBar(this, new UpdateStatusEventArgs(""));
			return true;
		}

		private void txtLLTemp_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!double.TryParse(txtLLTemp.Text, out var result))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidTemperatureValue, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return;
			}
			DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcThis[9] = true;
			DistillationMapObj.m_ReCalcEnvelope = true;
			this.LLTempChanged(this, new LLTempChangedEventArgs(result, LLTempUnits));
		}

		private void txtPressure_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!double.TryParse(txtPressure.Text, out var result) || result <= 0.0)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidPressureValue, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private void txtPressure_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (e.Key == Key.Return && (!double.TryParse(txtPressure.Text, out var result) || result <= 0.0))
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(Window.GetWindow(this), WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidPressureValue, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Input, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private void txtPressure_PreviewTextInput(object sender, TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = txtPressure.SelectionStart;
			if (e.Text[0] == '-')
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (!double.TryParse((txtPressure.Text + e.Text).Trim(), out var _))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}

		private void mnuAdvInput_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AdvInputDlg advInputDlg = new AdvInputDlg();
			advInputDlg.Owner = Window.GetWindow(this);
			advInputDlg.EnvStep = DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.StepSizeEnv;
			advInputDlg.CurveStep = DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.StepSizeCurve;
			advInputDlg.MaxIter = DistillationMapObj.m_MaxIter;
			advInputDlg.Tolerance = DistillationMapObj.m_FlashTol;
			int curveCalcOption = DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.CurveCalcOption;
			if (curveCalcOption == 2)
			{
				advInputDlg.Algorithm = advInputDlg.AlgorithmList[0];
			}
			else
			{
				advInputDlg.Algorithm = advInputDlg.AlgorithmList[1];
			}
			bool? flag = advInputDlg.ShowDialog();
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.StepSizeEnv = advInputDlg.EnvStep;
				DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.StepSizeCurve = advInputDlg.CurveStep;
				DistillationMapObj.m_MaxIter = advInputDlg.MaxIter;
				DistillationMapObj.m_FlashTol = advInputDlg.Tolerance;
				curveCalcOption = ((!(advInputDlg.Algorithm == "Standard")) ? 1 : 2);
				if (!IsAzeotropesChecked)
				{
					curveCalcOption = 2;
				}
				DistillationMapObj.m_IDistillationMap.CurveCalcOption = curveCalcOption;
			}
		}

		private void radioMassFraction_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis = 1;
			DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
		}

		private void radioMoleFraction_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			DistillationMapObj.m_nBasis = 2;
			DistillationMapObj.m_bInputChanged = true;
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}
