	public static class ImageResources
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, ImageSource> _imageSources = new Dictionary<string, ImageSource>();

		public static ImageSource Circle => GetImageSource("Circle.bmp");

		public static ImageSource CircleFill => GetImageSource("CircleFill.bmp");

		public static ImageSource CrisCross => GetImageSource("CrisCross.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Cross => GetImageSource("Cross.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Dashed => GetImageSource("Dashed.bmp");

		public static ImageSource DiagLeft => GetImageSource("DiagLeft.bmp");

		public static ImageSource DiagRight => GetImageSource("DiagRight.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Diamond => GetImageSource("Diamond.bmp");

		public static ImageSource DiamondFill => GetImageSource("DiamondFill.bmp");

		public static ImageSource DotDashed => GetImageSource("Dot-Dashed.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Dot1 => GetImageSource("Dot1.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Dot2 => GetImageSource("Dot2.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Dot3 => GetImageSource("Dot3.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Dotted => GetImageSource("Dotted.bmp");

		public static ImageSource HorizVert => GetImageSource("HorizVert.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Normal => GetImageSource("Normal.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Solid => GetImageSource("Solid.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Square => GetImageSource("Square.bmp");

		public static ImageSource SquareFill => GetImageSource("SquareFill.bmp");

		public static ImageSource StripeHoriz => GetImageSource("StripeHoriz.bmp");

		public static ImageSource StripeVert => GetImageSource("StripeVert.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Thick1 => GetImageSource("Thick1.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Thick2 => GetImageSource("Thick2.bmp");

		public static ImageSource Thick3 => GetImageSource("Thick3.bmp");

		public static ImageSource TriDown => GetImageSource("TriDown.bmp");

		public static ImageSource TriDownFill => GetImageSource("TriDownFill.bmp");

		public static ImageSource TriUp => GetImageSource("TriUp.bmp");

		public static ImageSource TriUpFill => GetImageSource("TriUpFill.bmp");

		public static ImageSource GetImageSource(string imageFileName)
		{
			if (!_imageSources.ContainsKey(imageFileName))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("pack://application:,,,/");
				stringBuilder.Append(typeof(ImageResources).Assembly.FullName);
				stringBuilder.Append(";component/Images/");
				stringBuilder.Append(imageFileName);
				_imageSources.Add(imageFileName, new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute)));
			}
			return _imageSources[imageFileName];
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI.Dialogs
{
	public class AddByValue : Window, INotifyPropertyChanged, IComponentConnector
	{
		public BasisType ABV_Basis;

		private string _selectedBasis;

		private const double dConValue = -999999.0;

		private double? _compVal0;

		private double? _compVal1;

		private double? _compVal2;

		private bool _isOKEnabled;

		internal Label lblCompName3;

		internal AesComboEdit cboBasis;

		internal Label lblCompName0;

		internal NumericTextBox txtCompVal0;

		internal Label lblCompName1;

		internal NumericTextBox txtCompVal1;

		internal Label lblCompName2;

		internal NumericTextBox txtCompVal2;

		internal Button btnOK;

		internal Button btncancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public string[] BasisList => new string[2]
		{
			WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MassFraction,
			WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.MoleFraction
		};

		public string SelectedBasis
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedBasis;
			}
			set
			{
				_selectedBasis = value;
				if (_selectedBasis.Equals(BasisList[0]))
				{
					ABV_Basis = BasisType.BT_Mass;
				}
				else
				{
					ABV_Basis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
				}
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedBasis");
			}
		}

		public double? CompVal0
		{
			get
			{
				if (_compVal0 == -999999.0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _compVal0;
			}
			set
			{
				_compVal0 = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("CompVal0");
				ValidateCompVals();
			}
		}

		public double? CompVal1
		{
			get
			{
				if (_compVal1 == -999999.0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _compVal1;
			}
			set
			{
				_compVal1 = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("CompVal1");
				ValidateCompVals();
			}
		}

		public double? CompVal2
		{
			get
			{
				if (_compVal2 == -999999.0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _compVal2;
			}
			set
			{
				_compVal2 = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("CompVal2");
				ValidateCompVals();
			}
		}

		public bool IsOKEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return _isOKEnabled;
			}
			set
			{
				_isOKEnabled = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsOKEnabled");
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		[DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, ExactSpelling = true)]
		private static extern IntPtr GetActiveWindow();

		public AddByValue(BasisType _basis, string _compName0, string _compName1, string _compName2, Window owner = null)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			ABV_Basis = _basis;
			lblCompName0.Content = _compName0;
			lblCompName1.Content = _compName1;
			lblCompName2.Content = _compName2;
			if (owner != null)
			{
				base.Owner = owner;
			}
			else
			{
				new WindowInteropHelper(this).Owner = GetActiveWindow();
			}
			base.Loaded += AddByValue_Loaded;
		}

		private void AddByValue_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DataContext = this;
			if (ABV_Basis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
			{
				SelectedBasis = BasisList[0];
			}
			else
			{
				SelectedBasis = BasisList[1];
			}
		}

		private void ValidateCompVals()
		{
			double? num = 0.0;
			if (CompVal0.HasValue)
			{
				num += CompVal0;
			}
			if (CompVal1.HasValue)
			{
				num += CompVal1;
			}
			if (CompVal2.HasValue)
			{
				num += CompVal2;
			}
			if (num > 1.0 || num < 1.0)
			{
				IsOKEnabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				IsOKEnabled = true;
			}
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		private void txtCompVal0_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (CompVal1.HasValue && CompVal2.HasValue)
			{
				double? num = 1.0 - (CompVal1 + CompVal2);
				if (num >= 0.0)
				{
					CompVal0 = num;
					IsOKEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SumGreater1, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ProductTitle, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				}
			}
		}

		private void txtCompVal1_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (CompVal0.HasValue && CompVal2.HasValue)
			{
				double? num = 1.0 - (CompVal0 + CompVal2);
				if (num >= 0.0)
				{
					CompVal1 = num;
					IsOKEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SumGreater1, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ProductTitle, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				}
			}
		}

		private void txtCompVal2_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (CompVal0.HasValue && CompVal1.HasValue)
			{
				double? num = 1.0 - (CompVal0 + CompVal1);
				if (num >= 0.0)
				{
					CompVal2 = num;
					IsOKEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SumGreater1, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ProductTitle, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				}
			}
		}

		private void AdvInputDlg_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DataContext = this;
			btnOK.IsEnabled = true;
			btnCancel.IsEnabled = true;
			btnDefaults.IsEnabled = true;

		public void LoadDefaults()
		{
			txtMaxIter.Text = "100";
			txtTol.Text = "0.0000001";
			cmdAlgorith.SelectedIndex = 0;
			txtCurveStep.Text = "0.3";
			txtEnvStep.Text = "0.1";
		}

		private void txtCurveStep_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			double result;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(txtCurveStep.Text))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.EnterAValue);
				_isCurveStepValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else if (!double.TryParse(txtCurveStep.Text, out result))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidValue, txtCurveStep.Text));
				_isCurveStepValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else if (result < 0.0)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.StepSizeNegative);
				_isCurveStepValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else
			{
				_isCurveStepValid = true;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
		}

		private void txtEnvStep_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			double result;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(txtEnvStep.Text))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.EnterAValue);
				_isEnvStepValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else if (!double.TryParse(txtEnvStep.Text, out result))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidValue, txtEnvStep.Text));
				_isEnvStepValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else if (result < 0.0 || result > 1.0)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.StepSizeBetween01);
				_isEnvStepValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else
			{
				_isEnvStepValid = true;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
		}

		private void txtMaxIter_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int result;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(txtMaxIter.Text))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.EnterAValue);
				_isMaxIterValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else if (!int.TryParse(txtMaxIter.Text, out result))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidValue, txtMaxIter.Text));
				_isMaxIterValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else if (result <= 0)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.EnterValidValue);
				_isMaxIterValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else
			{
				txtMaxIter.Text = result.ToString();
				_isMaxIterValid = true;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
		}

		private void txtTol_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			double result;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(txtTol.Text))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.EnterAValue);
				_isFlashTolValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else if (!double.TryParse(txtTol.Text, out result))
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InvalidValue, txtTol.Text));
				_isFlashTolValid = false;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
			else
			{
				txtTol.Text = Math.Abs(result).ToString();
				_isFlashTolValid = true;
				UpdateOKButton();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateOKButton()
		{
			btnOK.IsEnabled = _isMaxIterValid && _isFlashTolValid && _isCurveStepValid && _isEnvStepValid;
		}

				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

			_isMaxIterValid = true;
			_isFlashTolValid = true;
			_isCurveStepValid = true;
			_isEnvStepValid = true;
			UpdateOKButton();
		}

	public class NumericHelper
	{
		private static string m_strDecimalSymbol = string.Empty;

		public static string DecimalSymbol
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_strDecimalSymbol))
				{
					m_strDecimalSymbol = GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_strDecimalSymbol))
					{
						m_strDecimalSymbol = ".";
					}
				}
				return m_strDecimalSymbol;
			}
		}

		private static string GetSystemDecimalSymbol()
		{
			return 0.1.ToString().Replace("0", string.Empty).Replace("1", string.Empty);
		}
	}
	public class NumericTextBox : TextBox
	{
		protected override void OnPreviewTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			int selectionStart = base.SelectionStart;
			string text = base.Text;
			char num = e.Text[0];
			if (num == '-' && selectionStart != 0)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (num == NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0] && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text.Contains(NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0]))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			bool num = (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift) != 0;
			char c = NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0];
			if (!num)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (c == '.')
				{
					flag = e.Key == Key.OemPeriod;
				}
				if (c == ',')
				{
					flag = e.Key == Key.OemComma;
				}
				if (e.Key == Key.Subtract || e.Key == Key.Decimal || flag || e.Key == Key.OemMinus || (e.Key >= Key.NumPad0 && e.Key <= Key.NumPad9) || (e.Key >= Key.D0 && e.Key <= Key.D9) || e.Key == Key.Tab)
				{
					if (e.Key == Key.Decimal && NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0] != '.')
					{
						if (e.OriginalSource is IInputElement inputElement)
						{
							inputElement.RaiseEvent(new TextCompositionEventArgs(e.Device, new TextComposition(InputManager.Current, inputElement, NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol))
							{
								RoutedEvent = UIElement.TextInputEvent
							});
							e.Handled = true;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						e.Handled = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(double), typeof(string))]
	public class StringConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && (double)value != -999999.0)
			{
				return value.ToString();
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			try
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)value))
				{
					return -999999;
				}
				if (new string[4]
				{
					"-",
					"-0",
					"-0" + NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0],
					"-" + NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0]
				}.Contains((string)value))
				{
					return null;
				}
				double result = -999999.0;
				if (double.TryParse((string)value, out result))
				{
					return result;
				}
				return -999999;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
	}

				}
			}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public PropertiesDlg(PropertyPages startPage, PropertiesType propType, bool coDisplay, bool calcBoilPoint, bool transparentLabels, int toolbarPos)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			PropStartPage = startPage;
			Properties = propType;
			IsGlobal1Checked = coDisplay;
			IsGlobal2Checked = calcBoilPoint;
			IsGlobal3Checked = transparentLabels;
			if (toolbarPos == 4)
			{
				ToolbarPosIndex = 3;
			}
			else
			{
				ToolbarPosIndex = toolbarPos;
			}
			tabCtrl1.SelectedIndex = (int)startPage;
			SetProperties();
			base.Loaded += PropertiesDlg_Loaded;
		}

		private void PropertiesDlg_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DataContext = this;
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboCurveRisidueLineColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboCurveDistillationLineColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboLineTieLineColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboLineUserLineColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboMarkerAzeoColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboMarkerUserColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboEnvelopesColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboOtherDistBoundColor);
			PropertiesHelper.FillColour(cboOtherIsoColor);
		}

		public void SetProperties()
		{
			LabelFont = Properties.PFont.PName;
			LabelSize = Properties.PFont.PSize;
			CurveRisidueColour = (int)Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour;
			CurveDistillationColour = (int)Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour;
			CurveResidueMarkerStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker;
			CurveDistillationMarkerStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker;
			CurveResidueMarkerSizeValue = Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize;
			CurveDistillationMarkerSizeValue = Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize;
			CurveResidueLineStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine;
			CurveDistillationLineStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine;
			MarkerAzeoColour = (int)Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour;
			MarkerUserColour = (int)Properties.PUserMarker.PColour;
			MarkerAzeoMarkerStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker;
			MarkerUserMarkerStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker;
			MarkerAzeoSizeValue = Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize;
			MarkerUserSizeValue = Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize;
			LineTieColour = (int)Properties.PTieLine.PColour;
			LineUserColour = (int)Properties.PUserLine.PColour;
			LineTieMarkerStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker;
			LineUserMarkerStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PUserLine.PMarker;
			LineVaporMarkerStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2;
			LineTieMarkerSizeValue = Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize;
			LineUserMarkerSizeValue = Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize;
			LineTieLineStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PTieLine.PLine;
			LineUserLineStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PUserLine.PLine;
			EnvColour = (int)Properties.PEnvelope.PColour;
			EnvPaternIndex = Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern;
			OtherDistBoundColor = (int)Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour;
			OtherIsoColor = (int)Properties.PIsoCurve.PColour;
			OtherDistBoundStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker;
			OtherIsoStyleIndex = (int)Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarker;
			OtherDistBoundSizeValue = Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize;
			OtherIsoSizeValue = Properties.PIsoCurve.PMarkerSize;
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		private void btnOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AjustSettings = AjustSettings.AdjustYes;
			Close();
		}

		private void btnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AjustSettings = AjustSettings.AdjustNo;
			Close();
		}

		private void btnDefault_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AjustSettings = AjustSettings.AdjustDefault;
			Close();
		}

				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.WpfAzeoAnalysisGUI;component/dialogs/propertiesdlg.xaml", UriKind.Relative);

			}
		}

	public class AzeoGUIGlobals
	{
		public static int g_WindowsCount;

		public static IHNode IAspenPlusRootNode;

		public static IAPPDF IAspenEngine;

		public static IHNode ComponentNode;

		public static IHNode PropertyNode;

		public static string g_szAppdfFile;

		public static bool g_bGenerateAppdf;

		public static int g_nRefPropertyPackage;

		public static bool g_bInputChanged;

		public static DistillationApp DistAnalysisInterface;

		public static readonly string[] gPressureUnits = new string[13]
		{
			"N/SQM", "PSI", "ATM", "LBF/SQFT", "BAR", "MMHG, TORR", "IN-WATER", "KG/SQCM", "KPA", "MM-WATER",
			"MBAR", "LB/FT-SQSEC", "LB/M-SQSEC"
		};

		public static int gPressureDef = 0;

		public static string gPressureValue = "101325";

		public static readonly string[] gTempUnits = new string[4] { "R", "F", "C", "K" };

		public static int gTempDef = 2;

		public static string gTempValue = "35";

		public static string User_Save_Dir;

		private const int LOCALE_SDECIMAL = 14;

		private const int LOCALE_USER_DEFAULT = 0;

		public static DistillationApp IDistillationApp
		{
			get
			{
				if (DistAnalysisInterface == null)
				{
					DCGetSpDistilAnalysis(out DistAnalysisInterface);
				}
				return DistAnalysisInterface;
			}
			set
			{
				DistAnalysisInterface = value;
			}
		}

		[DllImport("MMUtil.Dll", CallingConvention = CallingConvention.StdCall)]
		public static extern void DCGetSpDistilAnalysis([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Interface)] out DistillationApp DistilAnalysis);

		public static void ReloadDistillationApp()
		{
			DCGetSpDistilAnalysis(out DistAnalysisInterface);
		}

		public static void ErrorBomb(string txt)
		{
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			MMMessageBox.Show(txt, WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ProductTitle, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
		}

		public static void ResetFlags()
		{
			DistAnalysisInterface = null;
			g_bGenerateAppdf = false;
		}

		public static int InitPropertySystem()
		{
			string currentDirectory = Directory.GetCurrentDirectory();
			int num = 0;
			if (!g_bGenerateAppdf)
			{
				IDistillationApp.CloseAppdfFile();
				if (IAspenPlusRootNode != null)
				{
					User_Save_Dir = CreateNewPath();
					try
					{
						string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(User_Save_Dir, "_as*.* ");
						foreach (string path in files)
						{
							try
							{
								File.Delete(path);
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
							}
						}
						string text = DateTime.Now.Minute.ToString() + DateTime.Now.Second + ".appdf";
						g_szAppdfFile = System.IO.Path.Combine(User_Save_Dir, "_as" + text);
						num = Get_APPDF(g_szAppdfFile, 0);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				else
				{
					g_szAppdfFile = System.IO.Path.Combine(User_Save_Dir, "_Split01.appdf");
					num = 1;
				}
				if (num <= 0)
				{
					g_bGenerateAppdf = false;
					return num;
				}
				try
				{
					IDistillationApp.InitFromAppdfFileWithCWD(g_szAppdfFile, currentDirectory);
					num = 1;
					g_bInputChanged = true;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.InitializeAppdfFailed);
					return -1;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = 1;
			}
			g_bGenerateAppdf = true;
			return num;
		}

		private static int Get_APPDF(string appdf_filename, int flag)
		{
			try
			{
				IHNode iHNode = IAspenPlusRootNode.Elements["Data", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing];
				if (((int)(dynamic)iHNode.get_AttributeValue((short)12, Type.Missing) & 0x40) != 0)
				{
					if (iHNode != null && iHNode.Elements["Streams", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing] != null && iHNode.Elements["Blocks", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing] != null)
					{
						int count = iHNode.Elements["Streams", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
						int count2 = iHNode.Elements["Blocks", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
						if (count == 0 && count2 == 0)
						{
							IAspenEngine.GetAPPDF(appdf_filename, flag);
							iHNode = null;
							return 1;
						}
					}
					ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.FlowsheetIncomplete);
					return 0;
				}
				IAspenEngine.GetAPPDF(appdf_filename, flag);
				iHNode = null;
				return 1;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GenerateAppdfFailed);
				return -1;
			}
		}

		private static string CreateNewPath()
		{
			return System.IO.Path.GetTempPath();
		}

		public static string CTemp(double dbTemp, string unit, bool showUnit = false)
		{
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "C":
				dbTemp = -273.15 + dbTemp;
				break;
			case "F":
				dbTemp = -459.67 + 1.8 * dbTemp;
				break;
			case "R":
				dbTemp = 1.8 * dbTemp;
				break;
			}
			string text = $"{dbTemp:0.00}";
			if (showUnit)
			{
				text = text + " " + unit;
			}
			return text;
		}

		public static double CTempDouble(double dbTemp, string unit)
		{
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "C":
				dbTemp = -273.15 + dbTemp;
				break;
			case "F":
				dbTemp = -459.67 + 1.8 * dbTemp;
				break;
			case "R":
				dbTemp = 1.8 * dbTemp;
				break;
			}
			return dbTemp;
		}

		public static double KTemp(double dbTemp, string unit)
		{
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "C":
				dbTemp = 273.15 + dbTemp;
				break;
			case "F":
				dbTemp = (459.67 + dbTemp) / 1.8;
				break;
			case "R":
				dbTemp /= 1.8;
				break;
			}
			return dbTemp;
		}

		public static double CPressure(double dbPress, string unit)
		{
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "PSI":
				dbPress /= 6894.7573;
				break;
			case "ATM":
				dbPress /= 101325.0;
				break;
			case "LBF/SQFT":
				dbPress /= 47.880259;
				break;
			case "BAR":
				dbPress /= 100000.0;
				break;
			case "MMHG, TORR":
				dbPress /= 133.3223684;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER":
				dbPress /= 249.0889;
				break;
			case "KG/SQCM":
				dbPress /= 98066.5;
				break;
			case "KPA":
				dbPress /= 1000.0;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER":
				dbPress /= 9.80665;
				break;
			case "MBAR":
				dbPress /= 100.0;
				break;
			case "LB/FT-SQSEC":
				dbPress /= 1.4881662;
				break;
			}
			return dbPress;
		}

		public static double KPressure(double dbPress, string unit)
		{
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "PSI":
				dbPress *= 6894.7573;
				break;
			case "ATM":
				dbPress *= 101325.0;
				break;
			case "LBF/SQFT":
				dbPress *= 47.880259;
				break;
			case "BAR":
				dbPress *= 100000.0;
				break;
			case "MMHG, TORR":
				dbPress *= 133.3223684;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER":
				dbPress *= 249.0889;
				break;
			case "KG/SQCM":
				dbPress *= 98066.5;
				break;
			case "KPA":
				dbPress *= 1000.0;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER":
				dbPress *= 9.80665;
				break;
			case "MBAR":
				dbPress *= 100.0;
				break;
			case "LB/FT-SQSEC":
				dbPress *= 1.4881662;
				break;
			}
			return dbPress;
		}

		public static void ConvertBasis(Way dir, ref double[] Composition_Array, ref double[] MolecWtArray, BasisType Baz, int nComp)
		{
			if (nComp > MolecWtArray.Length || nComp > Composition_Array.Length)
			{
				throw new IndexOutOfRangeException();
			}
			try
			{
				if (dir == Way.W_In)
				{
					if (Baz != BasisType.BT_Mass)
					{
						return;
					}
					double num = 0.0;
					for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
					{
						num += Composition_Array[num2] * MolecWtArray[num2];
					}
					if (num != 0.0)
					{
						for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
						{
							Composition_Array[num2] = Composition_Array[num2] * MolecWtArray[num2] / num;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (Baz != BasisType.BT_Mole)
					{
						return;
					}
					double num = 0.0;
					for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
					{
						num += Composition_Array[num2] / MolecWtArray[num2];
					}
					if (num == 0.0)
					{
						return;
					}
					for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
					{
						if (MolecWtArray[num2] != 0.0)
						{
							Composition_Array[num2] = Composition_Array[num2] / MolecWtArray[num2] / num;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ErrorBomb(string.Format(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.ConvertBasisError, ex.Message));
			}
		}

		private static double[] ChangeToMole(double[] CompostionArray, double[] MolarWghtArray)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				num += CompostionArray[num2] / MolarWghtArray[num2];
			}
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				CompostionArray[num2] = CompostionArray[num2] * num / MolarWghtArray[num2];
			}
			return CompostionArray;
		}

		public static double[] ChangeToMass(double[] CompostionArray, double[] MolarWghtArray)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				num += CompostionArray[num2] * MolarWghtArray[num2];
			}
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				CompostionArray[num2] = CompostionArray[num2] * MolarWghtArray[num2] / num;
			}
			return CompostionArray;
		}

		public static double ChangeFlowBasis(double dbflow, double[] MolarWght, double[] MoleFraction)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 <= 2; num2++)
			{
				num += dbflow * MoleFraction[num2] * MolarWght[num2];
			}
			return num;
		}

		public static double CMassFlowRate(double dbflow, string flowunit)
		{
			double result = dbflow;
			switch (flowunit)
			{
			case "KG/HR":
				result = dbflow * 3600.0;
				break;
			case "MTO/DAY":
				result = dbflow * 86.4;
				break;
			case "MTO/YEAR":
				result = dbflow * 31536.0;
				break;
			case "LB/S":
				result = dbflow * 2.2046;
				break;
			case "LB/HR":
				result = dbflow * 2.2046 * 3600.0;
				break;
			case "KG/MIN":
				result = dbflow * 60.0;
				break;
			case "LB/MIN":
				result = dbflow * 2.2046 * 60.0;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double KMassFlowRate(double dbflow, string flowunit)
		{
			double result = dbflow;
			switch (flowunit)
			{
			case "KG/HR":
				result = dbflow / 3600.0;
				break;
			case "MTO/DAY":
				result = dbflow / 86.4;
				break;
			case "MTO/YEAR":
				result = dbflow / 31536.0;
				break;
			case "LB/S":
				result = dbflow / 2.2046;
				break;
			case "LB/HR":
				result = dbflow / 7936.56;
				break;
			case "KG/MIN":
				result = dbflow / 60.0;
				break;
			case "LB/MIN":
				result = dbflow / 132.276;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double KMoleFlowRate(double dbflow, string flowunit)
		{

				result = dbflow / 60.0;
				break;
			case "kmol/hr":
				result = dbflow / 3600.0;
				break;
			case "tmol/day":
				result = dbflow / 86.4;
				break;
			case "lbmol/s":
				result = dbflow / 2.204;
				break;
			case "lbmol/min":
				result = dbflow / 132.28;
				break;
			case "lbmol/hr":
				result = dbflow / 7936.6;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		{

				result = dbflow / 60.0;
				break;
			case "kmol/hr":
				result = dbflow / 3600.0;
				break;
			case "tmol/day":
				result = dbflow / 86.4;
				break;

				result = dbflow / 2.204;
				break;
			case "lbmol/min":
				result = dbflow / 132.28;
				break;

		}

		public static string GetInstallationDir()
		{
			try
			{
				return Registry.GetValue("HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\\SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\Aspen Split\\" + ASplitVersion.ASPENSPLIT_VERSION_STRING, "SPLITDIR", "c:\\Split").ToString() + "\\Distillation Analysis";
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return "C:\\Splilt\\Distillation Analysis";
			}
		}

		public static string GetStabilityString(long stability)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			long num = stability - 1;
			if ((ulong)num <= 2uL)
			{
				switch (num)
				{
				case 0L:
					result = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.StableNode;
					break;
				case 1L:
					result = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.Saddle;
					break;
				case 2L:
					result = WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.UnstableNode;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class CAzeotropeSearch : ISplitDataObj
	{
		public AzeotropeSearch m_IAzeotropeSearch;

		public PropertyPackage PropPackage;

		public int[] m_vCompStability;

		public string[] m_vCompIDs;

		public double[,] m_vCompPoints;

		public double[] m_vCompTemps;

		public double[] m_vCompMole;

		public string[] m_sCompDescriptions;

		public bool m_bValidationError;

		public override int WhatAmI()
		{
			return 1;
		}

		public int GetAzeotropes()
		{
			int result = 1;
			object vName = null;
			if (!m_bInputChanged)
			{
				return result;
			}
			AzeoGUIGlobals.ResetFlags();
			result = AzeoGUIGlobals.InitPropertySystem();
			if (result == -1 || result == 0)
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (PropPackage == null)
			{
				try
				{
					PropPackage = AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp.GetPropertyPackage();
					AzeoGUIGlobals.g_nRefPropertyPackage++;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb("Failed to initialize property system.");
					return -1;
				}
			}
			try
			{
				object vComponents = m_vCompIDs;
				m_IAzeotropeSearch.SetComponentData(ref vComponents);
				m_IAzeotropeSearch.Pressure = AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits);
				m_IAzeotropeSearch.SetPropertyData(m_szLLEModel, m_szLLEModel, m_szLLEModel, m_nPhase);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb("Failed to set property data.");
				return -1;
			}
			try
			{
				object vCompIds = m_szComp;
				object vMolwt = m_vMole;
				PropPackage.GetComponentList(ref vCompIds, ref vName, ref vMolwt);
				m_szComp = vCompIds as string[];
				m_vMole = vMolwt as double[];
				for (int i = 0; i < m_szComp.Length; i++)
				{
					if (m_szComp[i].EndsWith("\0"))
					{
						m_szComp[i] = m_szComp[i].Substring(0, m_szComp[i].Length - 1);
					}
					m_szComp[i] = m_szComp[i].Trim();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb("Failed to set component list.");
				return -1;
			}
			try
			{
				m_IAzeotropeSearch.GetPureProperties(out var vComposition, out var vTemperature, out var vStability);
				m_vCompPoints = vComposition as double[,];
				m_vCompTemps = vTemperature as double[];
				m_vCompStability = vStability as int[];
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb("Failed to get pure properties.");
				return -1;
			}
			try
			{
				m_IAzeotropeSearch.ComputeAzeotropes();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb("Failed to compute azeotropes.");
				return -1;
			}
			try
			{
				m_IAzeotropeSearch.GetAzeotropes(out m_nAzeotropes, out var vAzeotropes, out var vTemperature2, out var vPhase, out var vStability2);
				m_vAzeotropes = vAzeotropes as double[,];
				m_vAzeoTemps = vTemperature2 as double[];
				m_vAzeoPhase = vPhase as int[];
				m_vAzeoStability = vStability2 as int[];
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb("Failed to get azeotropes from the calculation engine.");
				return -1;
			}
			return 1;
		}
	}

	}

	public class CDistillationMap : ISplitDataObj
	{
		public DistillationMap m_IDistillationMap;

		public PropertyPackage PropPackage;

		public bool DontDoItAgain;

		public double m_dbTemp;

		public object m_sCompDescriptions;

		public int m_nCurvePoints;

		public double[,,] m_vCurve;

		public int[] m_vCurveDir;

		public Curve CurveType;

		public int m_nIsoCurvePoints;

		public double[,,] m_vIsoCurve;

		public double m_dbPlaitTemp;

		public string[] m_vPureCompNames = new string[3];

		public double[,] m_vPureComp;

		public double[] m_vPureTemps;

		public int[] m_vPureStability;

		public double[] m_vPureMole = new double[3];

		public bool[] m_CalcThis = new bool[11];

		public bool[] m_ReCalcThis = new bool[11];

		public bool m_bValidationError;

		public bool m_PlottedBefore;

		public bool m_ReCalcTernary;

		public bool m_ReCalcEnvelope;

		public bool m_ReCalcEverything;

		public bool m_TernaryMapInit;

		public bool m_PressureChanged;

		public bool m_VLLEModelChanged;

		public bool m_LLEModelChanged;

		public double m_FlashTol = 1E-07;

		public int m_MaxIter = 100;

		public override int WhatAmI()
		{
			return 2;
		}

		public int GenerateTernaryMap()
		{
			int num = 1;
			if (!m_bInputChanged)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			switch (AzeoGUIGlobals.InitPropertySystem())
			{
			case -1:
				return -1;
			case 0:
				return 0;
			default:
			{
				if (PropPackage == null)
				{
					try
					{
						PropPackage = AzeoGUIGlobals.IDistillationApp.GetPropertyPackage();
						AzeoGUIGlobals.g_nRefPropertyPackage++;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetPropPackageFailed);
						return -1;
					}
				}
				PropPackage.FlashMaxIter = m_MaxIter;
				PropPackage.FlashTol = m_FlashTol;
				try
				{
					m_IDistillationMap.Pressure = AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits);
					m_IDistillationMap.Temperature = AzeoGUIGlobals.KTemp(m_dbTemp, m_sTempUnits);
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(ex2.Message);
					return -1;
				}
				try
				{
					m_IDistillationMap.SetComponentData(m_vPureCompNames[0], m_vPureCompNames[1], m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.SetComponentDataFailed);
					return -1;
				}
				object vName = new double[20];
				try
				{
					object vCompIds = m_szComp;
					object vMolwt = m_vMole;
					PropPackage.GetComponentList(ref vCompIds, ref vName, ref vMolwt);
					m_szComp = vCompIds as string[];
					for (int i = 0; i < m_szComp.Length; i++)
					{
						if (m_szComp[i].EndsWith("\0"))
						{
							m_szComp[i] = m_szComp[i].Substring(0, m_szComp[i].Length - 1);
						}
						m_szComp[i] = m_szComp[i].Trim();
					}
					m_vMole = vMolwt as double[];
					m_IDistillationMap.SetPropertyData(m_szVLEModel, m_szVLEModel, m_szLLEModel, m_nPhase);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetComponentListFailed);
					return -1;
				}
				try
				{
					m_IDistillationMap.GetPureProperties(out var vPureComp, out var vTemperature, out var vStability);
					m_vPureComp = vPureComp as double[,];
					m_vPureTemps = vTemperature as double[];
					m_vPureStability = vStability as int[];
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetPurePropertiesFailed);
					return -1;
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
				{
					m_vPureMole[j] = GetMoleWt(m_vPureCompNames[j]);
				}
				for (int k = 0; k <= 8; k++)
				{
					if (m_CalcThis[k])
					{
						if (!m_ReCalcThis[k])
						{
							continue;
						}
						switch (k)
						{
						case 0:
							try
							{
								m_IDistillationMap.GetAzeotropes(out m_nAzeotropes, out var vAzeotropes, out var vTemperature2, out var vPhase, out var vStability2);
								m_vAzeotropes = vAzeotropes as double[,];
								m_vAzeoTemps = vTemperature2 as double[];
								m_vAzeoPhase = vPhase as int[];
								m_vAzeoStability = vStability2 as int[];
								if (m_vAzeotropes == null)
								{
									AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetAzeotropesFailed);
									m_CalcThis[0] = false;
								}
								m_ReCalcThis[k] = false;
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
								AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetAzeotropesFailed);
								m_CalcThis[0] = false;
							}
							break;
						case 1:
							try
							{
								m_IDistillationMap.GetDistillationBoundary(out m_nCurvePoints, out var vDirection, out var vDistillationBoundary);
								m_vCurve = vDistillationBoundary as double[,,];
								m_vCurveDir = vDirection as int[];
								if (m_vCurve == null)
								{
									AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetDistillationBoundardyFailed);
									m_CalcThis[1] = false;
								}
								m_ReCalcThis[k] = false;
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
								AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetDistillationBoundardyFailed);
								m_CalcThis[1] = false;
							}
							break;
						case 2:
							try
							{
								m_IDistillationMap.GetIsovolatilityCurve(out m_nIsoCurvePoints, out var vIsovolatilityCurve);
								m_vIsoCurve = vIsovolatilityCurve as double[,,];
								if (m_vIsoCurve == null)
								{
									AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetIsovolatilityCurveFailed);
									m_CalcThis[2] = false;
								}
								m_ReCalcThis[k] = false;
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
								AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(WpfAzeoAnalysisGUIResources.GetIsovolatilityCurveFailed);
								m_CalcThis[2] = false;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						m_ReCalcThis[k] = true;
					}
				}
				return 1;
			}
			}
		}

		public double GetMoleWt(string sComp)
		{
			if (sComp.EndsWith("\0"))
			{
				sComp = sComp.Substring(0, sComp.Length - 1);
			}
			string[] szComp = m_szComp;
			for (int i = 0; i < szComp.Length; i++)
			{
				if (szComp[i].Trim().Equals(sComp))
				{
					return m_vMole[i];
				}
			}
			return 0.0;
		}
	}

		}

		public static readonly int SW_SHOWNORMAL = 1;

		public static readonly Collection<System.Windows.Media.Color> BuiltInColors = new Collection<System.Windows.Media.Color>
		{
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(0, 0, 0),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(64, 64, 192),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(0, 128, 0),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(byte.MaxValue, 64, 128),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(192, 64, 192),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(128, 128, 128),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(32, 160, 160),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(192, 192, 192),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(192, 192, 0),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(0, 0, byte.MaxValue),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(0, byte.MaxValue, 0),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(byte.MaxValue, 0, 0),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(byte.MaxValue, 0, byte.MaxValue),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(224, 224, 224),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(0, byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue, 0),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue),
			System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(byte.MaxValue, 128, 0)
		};

		public static void FillColour(XamColorPicker myColorPicker)
		{
			ColorPalette colorPalette = new ColorPalette();
			for (int i = 0; i <= 9; i++)
			{
				colorPalette.Colors.Add(BuiltInColors[i]);
			}
			ColorPalette colorPalette2 = new ColorPalette();
			for (int j = 10; j < BuiltInColors.Count; j++)
			{
				colorPalette2.Colors.Add(BuiltInColors[j]);
			}
			myColorPicker.ColorPalettes.Clear();
			myColorPicker.ColorPalettes.Add(colorPalette);
			myColorPicker.ColorPalettes.Add(colorPalette2);
		}

		public static int GetColorIndex(System.Windows.Media.Color selectedColor)
		{
			int num = BuiltInColors.IndexOf(selectedColor);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Color GetColorByIndex(int selectedColorIndex)
		{
			if (selectedColorIndex >= 0 && selectedColorIndex < BuiltInColors.Count)
			{
				return BuiltInColors[selectedColorIndex];
			}
			return BuiltInColors[0];
		}

		public static long StartURL(string URL)
		{
			Process.Start(URL);
			return 0L;
		}
